# Golden regression log

Append-only record of every change to the golden files in this folder. Entries are written by
`npm run golden:update -- --reason "…"`; do not edit or reorder past entries.

## 2026-09-28 13:42 UTC — ws3 1.5D integrity: Grad-Shafranov updates that did not converge were dropped silently (JET15 14 of 17, DIIID15 5 of 9, DEMO15 5 of 30, MASTU15 7 of 7) and are now retried (relaxation 0.5, then the pressure table filtered at the GS grid scale with relaxation 0.3) and accepted (JET15 17/17, DIIID15 9/9, DEMO15 30/30, MASTU15 6 accepted + 4 rejected and reported), so the transport geometry follows the plasma: JET15, DIIID15, DEMO15, MASTU15 move. All 1.5D cases gain the report counts 'GS updates accepted / needing a retry / rejected', 'Forced transport steps' and the diagnostic tauE_scal. The work-array refresh after an equilibrium swap, explicit step-retry exhaustion, the full rewind checkpoint, cgm tau_E = W/P_loss and the initial-GS guard move no golden number (ITER15 and the SPARC15 cases only gain keys).

## 2026-09-28 14:05 UTC — ws2b 0D physics fixes: separate alpha/beam fast-ion pools (P_alpha = charged-product heating only, new P_beam_heat; ignition = P_alpha >= P_rad + W/tau_E without the Q>=5 guard; fast-particle pressure in beta_T/beta_N, NTM seeding on thermal beta_N); D-D with fuelFracA<1 and D-3He D-D side channels (0D, pulsed, POPCON; 1.5D picks up the D-3He channels); ICF burn with the selected fuel (H_B, energy and neutrons per reaction, ignition scale) and Q_eng = G eta_driver eta_th; tandem-mirror Pastukhov plug factor; heating.autoOff; Greenwald, Martin L-H (plus Ryter low-density branch) and tau_E scalings at the line-averaged density; P_L = P_heat - P_rad,core(rho<0.6) - smoothed dW/dt for tau_E and L-H; ELM-averaged power 0.3 W/tau_E instead of 0.3 P_heat; sawtooth crashes conserve W and particles; blanket multiplication on the neutron share only; e-i equilibration over all ions with computed lnLambda; Sauter q95 for spherical tokamaks; explicit H_ISS04; T_max and score exclude the start-up; MagLIF stagnation 2 ns; FRC/mirror W = 3/2 (n_e + n_i) T V. Shared report changes (T_max window, blanket economics) also move the 1.5D cases.

## 2026-09-28 14:26 UTC — ws4 (Grad-Shafranov): table mode meets I_p through FF' only (p' was rescaled with FF', leaving the reported p, beta_p and W_th 9-25 % out of force balance), closed-form beta0 (beta0 > 1 allowed), bounded 8-layer exterior fill + Jacobi, interior-only LU, Anderson-accelerated Picard and a saddle-free axis search. The ws4 re-record of 13:51 was reverted and is redone here with the full list of moves (the files are byte-identical to it). 0D cases unchanged. Two mechanisms move the 9 1.5D cases: (i) the field no longer holds c*p' while reporting p' (c = 0.99-1.07, typically 1.02, in the accepted solves); (ii) more table-mode updates converge and are accepted, so the transport geometry history changes (equilibrium frames JET15 4->7, DIIID15 5->7, DEMO15 26->31, MASTU15 1->2). Flat-top averages unless noted. ITER15: Q 9.755->9.766, q95 3.475->3.473, li 0.7255->0.7266, beta_p 0.5627->0.5630, qmin 0.987->0.978, rho_q1 0.088->0.180 (x2), sawteeth 25->21 with period 16.0->19.05 s, ELMs 1022->1023, Shafranov shift 0.170->0.165 m, dW/dt +4.6 %, V_loop +3.4 %; last equilibrium beta_p 0.574->0.568. JET15: Q 0.4355->0.4391, q95 3.699->3.688, li 0.7636->0.7615, beta_p 0.6272->0.6282, q0 0.983->1.185 and qmin 0.983->1.046, so the flat-top has no q = 1 surface any more (rho_q1 -0.355->-1), Shafranov shift 0.146->0.100 m (the last accepted equilibrium is now a flat-top one at beta_p 0.63 instead of a ramp-up one at 0.45), S_fuel -18.5 %, V_loop -8.9 %, f_bs +2.1 %. SPARC15: flat-top within 0.9 % (rho_q1 and dW/dt -0.9 %, Q 6.088->6.087, q95 4.163->4.162, all others <= 0.24 %). SPARC15-short: V_loop +1.3 %, all others <= 0.5 %. SPARC15-DHe3: rho_q1 -0.466->-0.453, all others <= 0.07 %. SPARC15-pB11: all <= 0.4 %. In the four SPARC cases the Shafranov shift moves by -0.6 to -1.7 % and the history V_loop minimum by 1.4-9.4 % (smaller in magnitude). DEMO15: Q 22.72->22.76, q95 4.920->4.925, li 0.7453->0.7451, beta_p 1.156->1.157, q0 1.478->1.665, Shafranov shift 0.403->0.439 m, dW/dt +27 %, V_loop +8.1 %, ELMs 1076->1068; last equilibrium beta_p 1.126->1.142. DIIID15: q95 5.931->5.908, li 0.8054->0.8043, q0 1.112->1.342, rho_q1 -0.58->-0.66, S_fuel +13 %, dW/dt +25 %, V_loop -2.8 %. MASTU15, a coupling transient: the old baseline never accepted a table equilibrium and kept the beta_p = 0.1 start-up geometry all shot (Shafranov shift 0.109 m at a flat-top beta_p of 1.15). Table solves 1-5 still stall at a residual of ~1e-2. Solve 6 at t = 1.4925 s now converges (the old code stopped at 3.5e-3) with currentScale 1.27: the <j_phi/R> and p tables, mapped to psi_N through the stale start-up equilibrium, carry 79 % of I_p on its flux surfaces, and its beta_p is 0.785 against 1.134 in transport. The 1.5D model accepts it (R_axis 0.959->1.063 m in one step) and keeps psi(rho) across the geometry change. In the next frame (t = 1.495 s) V_loop goes +0.045->-2.293 V, P_oh 0.006->0.513 MW (x85) and li 0.716->0.751, and the induced negative field reverses the core ohmic current for the rest of the shot. History V_loop min -0.0115->-2.293 V, P_oh max 0.195->0.513 MW. Flat-top: q0 2.38->9.51, q95 19.70->16.87, li 0.7259->0.7182, beta_p 1.149->1.160, P_oh x4.1, P_neutron +35 %, S_fuel -25 %, dW/dt +0.016->-0.036 MW. Last profile: johm[0] +0.072->-0.838 MA/m2, q[0] 1.73->8.42. Shafranov shift 0.109->0.213 m. The accepted state is a correct equilibrium of the tables it was given (force-balance residual 1.1e-5); the transient comes from a stale geometry catching up in one step. GS now flags such states with an Equilibrium.warnings 'table-current-rescaled' entry (|currentScale - 1| > 0.1; every other accepted solve in these cases has |c - 1| <= 0.067). Gating or relaxing these updates in model.ts is a required ws3 request, and MASTU15 must be re-recorded once it lands: rejecting them restores the old trajectory (V_loop min -0.012 V, P_oh max 0.195 MW).

## 2026-09-28 19:51 UTC — ws3 addendum (no golden file changed): headline numbers and causes for the two ws3 entries above (13:42 stage A, 15:21 stage C); the 15:21 entry understates the stage C moves

Node v24.19.0 · `npm run golden:update` was not run for this entry · numbers compare the golden files before and after each entry (fork point 3d04e96, stage A d57f456, stage C 933b559); the geometry / sawtooth split comes from a snapshot of the 1.5D cases recorded at the geometry-fix commit 4894a8b alone

- Stage A (13:42 entry). Cause: Grad-Shafranov updates that did not converge are retried and accepted, so the transport geometry follows the plasma. JET15: Q_sci_max 1.231 -> 1.314, E_fusion 84.57 -> 85.08 MJ, flat-top Q 0.4355 -> 0.4409, P_fus 14.44 -> 14.62 MW, f_bs 0.240 -> 0.247, Shafranov shift 0.146 -> 0.096 m, burn time 0.241 -> 0.220 s, equilibrium frames 4 -> 18. DEMO15: flat-top Q 22.721 -> 22.714, ELMs 1076 -> 1077, last-equilibrium q95 4.840 -> 4.898, equilibrium frames 26 -> 31. DIIID15: ELMs 250 -> 252, f_bs 0.3094 -> 0.3107, last-equilibrium l_i 0.800 -> 0.836, final T_ped 0.68 -> 0.51 keV, equilibrium frames 5 -> 10. MASTU15 (the equilibrium used to stay the initial beta_p = 0.1 one for the whole shot): f_bs 0.541 -> 0.396, l_i(3) 0.726 -> 0.832, flat-top q95 19.7 -> 17.6, last-equilibrium beta_p 0.10 -> 1.59, Shafranov shift 0.109 -> 0.216 m, Q_sci_max 3.12e-4 -> 2.97e-4, score 28 -> 27, warnings 1 -> 2. ITER15 and the SPARC15 cases only gained keys.
- Stage C (15:21 entry), causes: (G) the transport-geometry cell volumes come from V(rho^2) with the V' end slopes (the innermost cell was 1.3 % too small; geometry.test.ts); (S) sawtooth crashes conserve the electron and ion energy exactly; (M) ion heat convected in through the separatrix scales with n_i/n_e (only MASTU15 has boundary inflow); P_bound is a diagnostic only. Moves from the stage A files:
  - Every 1.5D case (G), flat-top central safety factor q(0): ITER15 1.061 -> 1.033 (-2.6 %; G alone -2.4 %), JET15 -2.4 %, DIIID15 -4.4 %, SPARC15 0.942 -> 0.909 (-3.6 %), SPARC15-short, -DHe3 and -pB11 -2.9 to -3.0 % (q_min = q(0) there), MASTU15 +0.1 %, DEMO15 1.470 -> 1.686 (+14.6 %; hollow current profile, q_min +0.13 %). The cell volumes of the first cells enter the current-diffusion balance (the psi inertia and source terms carry Delta V, and <j.B> is the flux divergence over Delta V), so a 1.3 % change of the central cell moves the current density near the axis; the flat-top mean of rho(q=1) follows (-1 where there is no q = 1 surface). The geometry-only snapshot reproduces these q(0) values.
  - ITER15 (G, then S): sawtooth count 25 -> 38, period 16 -> 10.5 s (the geometry commit alone gives 39). The shear at q = 1 reaches sawtoothShear earlier in the q_min decay once q(0) has moved (q(0) at t = 60 s, before the first crash, 1.164 -> 1.134 with q_min unchanged; crashes at s_1 = 0.22-0.30 instead of 0.30-0.35). The count is stable within each version (37-39 now, 24-26 before, for P_aux +0.2 %, I_p +0.1 % and two other RNG seeds), so this is a systematic shift, not noise. Flat-top: T_e(0) 23.98 -> 23.85 keV (-0.52 %: G -0.75 %, S +0.23 %), Q 9.7545 -> 9.7495, P_fus 490.6 -> 490.4 MW, l_i -0.19 %, P_SOL -0.11 %, P_rad -0.16 %, P_sync -0.7 %, T_ped +0.23 %, V_loop 0.0624 -> 0.0642 V (+2.8 %; the flat-top is a frame-weighted mean, so it includes the V_loop spikes of the extra frames at each crash and follows the crash count); E_fusion and Q_sci_avg -0.42 %, Q_sci_max -0.12 %; ELMs 1022 -> 1024, ELM frequency 2.61 -> 2.55 Hz; end-of-shot T_ped 4.53 -> 4.36 keV (ELM-cycle phase).
  - JET15 (G): Q_sci_max 1.3144 -> 1.3200 (+0.42 %), burn time 0.2200 -> 0.2131 s, triple product max +0.27 %, T_i max +0.22 %; flat-top: S_fuel +1.7 %, burn fraction -0.23 %, V_loop -0.11 %, every other value within 1e-3 (apart from q(0) and the near-zero mean dW/dt).
  - DEMO15 (G): flat-top V_loop 0.0122 -> 0.0148 V (+21 %; a 12 mV quantity, P_oh +0.66 %), Q 22.714 -> 22.758 (+0.19 %), P_SOL +0.65 %, T_ped -1.4 %, S_fuel +1.0 %, Q_sci_max 23.449 -> 23.552 (+0.44 %), Shafranov shift (last equilibrium) 0.402 -> 0.448 m, GS updates needing a retry 5 -> 2, ELMs 1077 -> 1078.
  - DIIID15 (G): flat-top V_loop -0.57 %, T_ped +0.23 %, alpha_ped +0.46 %, l_i +0.10 %, the cumulative E_fusion and E_in +1.0 % (frame means), scalar E_fusion +0.18 %.
  - MASTU15 (G, M): flat-top P_oh -1.4 %, S_fuel +1.3 %, V_loop -0.16 %, f_bs -0.09 %, neutron yield +0.19 %; (M) moves the ramp-up history means by up to 0.25 % (447 heat solves between 0.05 and 0.31 s) and the run takes one step fewer.
  - SPARC15, SPARC15-short, SPARC15-DHe3, SPARC15-pB11 (G): q(0) as above; every other flat-top value within 2e-4 and every scalar within 1.1e-4 (the 'keys moved' count is profile and history-statistic keys).
  - Keys added in every 1.5D case: P_bound (history statistics and flat-top). The 15:21 entry lists equilibrium.last.* among the added keys; the key sets of the files before and after differ by the six P_bound keys only.

## 2026-09-28 20:03 UTC — ws2b final re-record: review fixes on top of the 13c11cb re-record, and the complete list of what the ws2b lane moved since the v3 baseline (3d04e96), with causes. (A) Since 13c11cb: P_cond is again the continuous conduction W/tau_E - P_ELM (H-mode cases -30 %; the energy ledger closes with it), new diagnostics P_transport = W/tau_E (whole transport loss, used by the ignition test) and P_ELM = 0.3 P_transport (evaluated in rhs from the current state, no one-step lag); no conduction loss from a trial stage with W < 0; evaluation temperature bounded to 0.01-1e4 keV; MagLIF dwell sigma = 0.6 t_c/CR_eff (Z unchanged: 2 ns at t_c = 100 ns, CR 30); non-negative state clamped in postStep. Flat-top values, Efus, Q and ELM counts move by less than 0.7 % (ELM 1312 -> 1310 on ITER); ITER-pB11 keeps its 27.9 s collapse. (B) All 0D physics moves since 3d04e96. ITER 0D (400 s): Q_sci_max 15.94 -> 13.69, flat-top Q 13.98 -> 10.42, flat-top P_fus 715.5 -> 538.3 MW, E_fus 270.2 -> 207.1 GJ (bisected: loss power P_L = P_heat - P_rad,core(rho < 0.6) - dW/dt is -38 % E_fus, ELM power 0.3 W/tau_E is -4 %, tau_E and L-H at the line-averaged density is +30 %); P_alpha 172.9 -> 106.1 MW (v3 counted ~32 MW of NBI in P_alpha; beams are now P_beam_heat); ignitionTime 385.6 -> 0 s and the spurious IGNITION event at 14.5 s is gone (50 MW of external heating is on); the H->L back-transition, one burn_end and two burn_start events are gone; Tmax 21.97 -> 20.12 keV (start-up transient excluded); beta_N 1.87 -> 1.70 (fast-ion pressure added, thermal 1.59); n/n_G 0.82 -> 0.92 (line average of the same 1.0e20 volume-average target); Q_eng 1.66 -> 1.27 (blanket multiplication on the neutron share only). JET: E_fus 58.3 -> 64.2 MJ, Q_sci_max 0.377 -> 0.455, P_alpha 31.0 -> 2.45 MW (beams 28.75 MW now separate), Tmax 7.40 -> 8.25 keV, beta_N 1.34 -> 1.63 (fast pressure), Q_eng 0.0334 -> 0.0323 (no blanket, no multiplication). SPARC: Q_sci_max 7.62 -> 8.62, flat-top Q 6.92 -> 6.64, E_fus 1361 -> 1465 MJ, flat-top P_fus 181.8 -> 174.7 MW, Q_eng 0.845 -> 0.707, one burn_end gone. DEMO (600 s): Q_sci_max 23.78 -> 22.43, P_fus 2193 -> 2078 MW, ignitionTime 575.9 -> 0 s (no ignition with NBI on), beta_N 2.27 -> 2.48 (thermal 2.19), n/n_G 1.11 -> 1.24 (limit 1.3), Q_eng 2.64 -> 2.43, burn_end and ignition events gone. DIII-D (D-D): P_alpha 11.66 -> 0.0023 MW (beams 11.67 MW separate), beta_N 1.88 -> 2.23, Tmax 5.03 -> 4.54 keV, E_fus 0.0219 -> 0.0204 MJ. JT-60SA: P_alpha 33.3 -> 0.079 MW, beta_N 2.03 -> 2.41, Tmax 11.1 -> 10.5 keV, E_fus unchanged. MAST-U: q95 34.3 -> 18.2 (Sauter low-aspect-ratio fit), P_alpha 4.55 -> 0.0004 MW, beta_N 3.39 -> 4.07, Tmax 2.69 -> 3.05 keV. W7-X: Tmax 2.26 -> 2.00 keV (start-up window), P_fus -4 % (core-radiation P_L, n-bar in ISS04; H_ISS04 alias keeps the confinement multiplier). ITER-DHe3: Tmax 17.8 -> 2.7 keV, tau_E 11.1 -> 4.2 s, E_fus 133 -> 24.8 MJ, neutrons 0 -> 1.3e18 (D-D side channels of D-3He); v3 clipped P_cond to 0 with an ELM term larger than the whole transport loss and inflated tau_E through the P_loss floor. (C) ITER-pB11 (ITER geometry, p-11B, 50 MW external heating, Z_eff 4.6) now ends in a radiative collapse at 27.9 s instead of the scheduled end at 100 s: stableTime 100 -> 27.87 s, E_fus 0.0464 -> 0.00507 MJ, ELMs 946 -> 33, score 40 -> 16. Cause: not a wrong change but the removal of a v3 artefact. The v3 ELM model took off an ELM-averaged power of 0.3 P_heat = 23.3 MW (5.5 times the whole transport loss W/tau_E = 4.2 MW; conduction clipped to 0) and a nominal particle exhaust of 0.132 1/s (4.9 times the total 1/tau_p = 0.027 1/s), which held the density at n/n_G = 0.72. With ELM losses that are a fraction (0.3) of W/tau_E, the density follows its 1.0e20 target, bremsstrahlung (Z_eff 4.6) and Be/Ar line radiation rise, and P_rad (84.3 MW) exceeds P_heat (83.8 MW) at T_e = 2 keV. The outcome is set by the density ramp, not the ELM model: without ELMs the run collapses too (density limit, 28.9 s), n_target 0.85e20 survives to 100 s, H98 +-10 % does not change it (regress/pb11Collapse.test.ts). (D) Other fuels and concepts. NIF: gain G 1.489 unchanged, Q_eng 0.0164 -> 0.0595 (drive coupling no longer counted twice); DIRECT Q_eng 0.186 -> 0.124. Z MagLIF: burn FWHM ~30 -> ~2 ns, DD neutrons 3.0e15 -> 2.0e14, G 1.17e-3 -> 7.8e-5 (still 10-100x above Gomez et al. 2020: lossless adiabatic compression). Tandem MIRROR: tau_E 8.25 -> 16.4 ms (Pastukhov plug factor 2.81 at plugPotential = 1), Tmax 5.0 -> 9.5 keV, Q_sci_max 2.4e-4 -> 1.8e-3. MIRROR-DHe3: E_fus x157 (9.1e-8 -> 1.4e-5 MJ), Tmax 5 -> 8.2 keV, neutrons 0 -> 5.7e12 (plug factor and D-D side channels). TAE-pB11: P_fus x10 (FRC energy content W = 3/2 (n_e + n_i) T V; D-T and D-D FRC/mirror bit-for-bit unchanged). SPARC15-DHe3: neutrons 0 -> 8.8e16 and P_fus +15 % (D-D side channels of D-3He in the 1.5D model). (E) 1.5D report-only moves (flat-top physics of the 1.5D model is unchanged): T_max and the design score no longer include the start-up transient: ITER15 Tmax 64.7 -> 28.2 keV, JET15 30.8 -> 12.1 (score 63 -> 53), SPARC15 67.2 -> 25.9, DEMO15 75.4 -> 41.6, DIIID15 13.7 -> 5.9 (score 34 -> 26), MASTU15 8.1 -> 3.8 (score 28 -> 24); Q_eng of the 1.5D cases falls 4-14 % because the blanket multiplies only the neutron share and only when one exists (ITER15 1.251 -> 1.195, SPARC15 0.758 -> 0.657, SPARC15-short 0.801 -> 0.693).

## 2026-09-28 20:43 UTC — merge v4/ws2b onto v4/integration (ws5, ws10, ws4, ws1b, ws9, ws2a already merged): re-record of the 1.5D cases only, where ws4 (Grad-Shafranov equilibrium) and ws2b (0D physics fixes) both re-recorded and the two sets of moves combine; every 0D, ICF, MTF, FRC, mirror and muon golden of ws2b matches bit for bit on the ws5 kernel and is unchanged here. (A) Report-only moves carried by ws2b, same values as in its 13c11cb/2c166b2 entries: T_max, Temax, Timax, Tmax_MC and the design score exclude the start-up transient (ITER15 Tmax 64.7 to 28.2 keV, JET15 30.8 to 12.1, SPARC15 67.2 to 25.9, DEMO15 75.4 to 41.6, DIIID15 13.7 to 5.9, MASTU15 8.1 to 3.8; scores JET15 63 to 53, DIIID15 34 to 26, MASTU15 28 to 24, SPARC15 85 to 83); Q_eng and the engineering.* keys fall 4 to 14 percent because blanket energy multiplication now applies to the neutron power share only and only if a blanket exists (ITER15 Q_eng 1.253 to 1.197, JET15 0.0354 to 0.0336, SPARC15 0.758 to 0.657, SPARC15-short 0.801 to 0.693, DEMO15 2.717 to 2.590). The 1.5D flat-top physics itself is unchanged in these cases. (B) SPARC15-DHe3 (2118 keys): the 1.5D model now picks up the D(d,p)T and D(d,n)3He side channels of the D-3He fuel that ws2b added to FUEL_CHANNELS, so the neutron yield goes from 0 to 8.8e16, P_fus and P_charged rise 13 to 15 percent, E_fus 0.988 to 1.096 MJ, and the profile, equilibrium and flat-top keys drift with the extra heating (T_e, T_i, W and beta up under 1 percent, equilibrium Raxis 2e-5, Shafranov shift 2e-3) on top of the ws4 equilibrium record. Not moved by this merge: SPARC15-pB11 and every 0D case. The 1.5D counterparts of the other 0D fixes (P_L with core radiation, Ryter branch, fast-ion pools, autoOff) are not in ws3 yet and land with that lane.

## 2026-09-28 21:08 UTC — merge v4/ws3 onto v4/integration (ws5, ws10, ws4, ws1b, ws9, ws2a, ws2b already merged) plus the integrator fixes on top: re-record of the 9 1.5D cases only; the 21 non-1.5D goldens match bit for bit. The ws3 entries above (13:42, 15:21, 19:51) give its moves against the v3.0.0 baseline; the numbers here are against the integration goldens of the 20:43 entry (ws4 + ws2b). Causes: (A) Grad-Shafranov updates that do not converge are retried (relaxation 0.5, then the pressure table filtered at the GS grid scale) and accepted, so the transport geometry follows the plasma; (G) transport-geometry cell volumes are splined from V(rho^2) with the V' end slopes (the innermost cell was 1.3 % too small), which moves the central q(0) by -2 to -4 % and the q = 1 timing; (S) sawtooth crashes conserve the electron and ion energy exactly; (M) MASTU15 ion heat convected in through the separatrix scales with n_i/n_e; (C) integrator: an update whose current table had to be rescaled by more than 50 % to meet I_p is rejected (CURRENT_SCALE_LIMIT; ws4's solver converges MASTU15 tables with currentScale 2.07 and 1.95 that ended the shot in a beta-limit disruption at 1.06 s). New keys in every 1.5D case: P_bound and tauE_scal (history statistics and flat-top), the report counts 'GS updates accepted / needing a retry / rejected' and 'Forced transport steps'. GS updates accepted/retried/rejected: ITER15 23/0/0, JET15 15/3/1, SPARC15 30/0/0, SPARC15-short 17/0/0, SPARC15-DHe3 18/0/0, SPARC15-pB11 14/0/0, DEMO15 30/1/0, DIIID15 9/1/0, MASTU15 2/2/5. Headline moves against the integration goldens (flat-top frame means unless noted). ITER15 (G, S): sawtooth crashes 21 -> 36, period 19.05 -> 11.11 s (G lowers q(0), so the shear at q = 1 reaches the crash threshold earlier; S makes each crash energy-neutral), q(0) 1.0643 -> 1.0393 (-2.35 %), q_min 0.978 -> 0.992, T_e(0) and T_i(0) -0.93 %, Q 9.766 -> 9.744, P_fus 491.1 -> 490.1 MW, V_loop -1.1 %, P_SOL -0.44 %, P_sync -1.3 %, dW/dt -1.3 %, E_fusion and Q_sci_avg -0.56 %, net electric power -2.9 %, ELMs 1023 -> 1020 (2.50 -> 2.46 Hz), last-equilibrium beta_p 0.568 -> 0.572; end-of-shot T_ped +2.5 % and T_sep +5.4 % are ELM-phase values; Q_sci_max 16.012 -> 16.001, score 95 unchanged. JET15 (A, G): equilibrium frames 7 -> 16 (15 updates accepted, 3 of them after a retry, 1 rejected and reported: the update at t = 0.676 s stalls in every ladder stage; warnings 3 -> 4), so the geometry follows the ramp-up: q(0) 1.185 -> 1.408 (+18.8 %), q_min 1.046 -> 1.120, V_loop +12.5 % (loop voltage average +11.9 %), S_fuel +28 %, f_bs +0.7 %, Q 0.4391 -> 0.4409 and P_fus 14.57 -> 14.63 MW (+0.41 %), T_e(0) +0.41 %, burn fraction -2.1 %, Q_sci_max 1.231 -> 1.249, E_fusion 84.76 -> 85.41 MJ, burn time 0.241 -> 0.248 s, Shafranov shift 0.100 -> 0.096 m, ELMs 148 -> 149, T_max 12.08 -> 12.27 keV, score 53 -> 54; end-of-shot T_ped -27 % (ELM phase). DEMO15 (G): q(0) 1.665 -> 1.815 (+9.0 %; hollow current, q_min moves +0.14 % only), V_loop +11 % (15.2 -> 16.8 mV; loop voltage average +13 %), P_SOL +0.59 %, P_oh +0.50 %, Q 22.756 -> 22.736, dW/dt -2.75 %, Q_sci_max 23.376 -> 23.445, ELMs 1068 -> 1072 (2.29 -> 2.23 Hz), last-equilibrium beta_p 1.142 -> 1.102, Shafranov shift 0.439 -> 0.396 m. DIIID15 (A, G): equilibrium frames 7 -> 10 (9 accepted, 1 after a retry), S_fuel -11.8 %, V_loop +1.2 %, q_min +2.0 %, q(0) -0.22 %, rho_q1 -0.66 -> -0.82, dW/dt +21 % (1.40 -> 1.69, a small difference of large terms), E_fusion and E_in (frame means) +0.8 %, T_max +1.0 %, everything else within 0.45 %, score 26 unchanged. SPARC15, SPARC15-short, SPARC15-DHe3, SPARC15-pB11 (G only): q(0) = q_min -3.65 %, -2.86 %, -2.95 %, -2.96 %; every other flat-top value within 2e-4 apart from rho_q1 (the q = 1 radius is 0.01-0.04 and moves with q(0)) and dW/dt, every scalar within 4e-4 except T_ped (final) of SPARC15-short (2e-3). MASTU15 (A, G, M, C), a documented poor case (hollow current at beta_p about 1 to 1.8 where the table-mode solve stalls; ws3 and ws4 requests): the shot now ends as scheduled instead of disrupting once the mis-scaled tables are held back; 2 updates are accepted (t = 0.13 and 0.25 s) and 5 rejected and reported (warnings 1 -> 2), so the transport geometry stays at the t = 0.25 s equilibrium for the remaining 1.75 s (last-equilibrium beta_p 0.785 -> 1.80, Shafranov shift 0.213 -> 0.319 m, q95 of that equilibrium 27.1). Against the ws4 baseline, which had accepted the c = 2 table at t = 1.49 s and recorded its transient: q(0) = q_min 9.51 -> 0.22 (the face-0 value of a current peaked on axis: the last q profile rises from 0.42 on axis to 1.5 at rho = 0.12, the q < 1 region is under one cell wide, no sawtooth), T_e(0) -20.6 %, Q -20.7 %, P_fus -23 %, S_fuel x4.6, neutron yield -69 %, V_loop -2.3 %, ELMs 203 -> 213, score 24 -> 22. MASTU15 must not be read as validated: only the absence of the disruption is intended; the follow-up is an outer iteration of the current table against the new geometry (ws4/ws3).

## 2026-09-28 21:47 UTC — ws5b: frames recorded at a type-I ELM crash of the 0D magnetic model now carry the diagnostics of their own (post-crash) state; before, postStep() cut W by about 3 % and the frame stored that y next to the cached diagnostics of the pre-crash state (MagneticModel.refreshDiagnostics re-evaluates them at the crash). The run itself is bitwise unchanged (y, events, counters E_fusion_MJ, neutronYield, burn and ignition times, ELM/LH/sawtooth times do not move; run digests of y are identical) and only the diagnostics of the ELM frames change, so the 9 cases with ELMs move where numbers are frame averages or frame extrema and nothing else: ITER, JET, SPARC, DIIID, JT60SA, MASTU, DEMO, ITER-DHe3, ITER-pB11. Unchanged: W7X (no ELMs), all 1.5D cases, all pulsed cases; the FSAL stage reuse and the kernel contract work of the same lane were checked separately and move nothing (golden green at that commit). Cause of the size: the frame-weighted flat-top mean includes the extra frames recorded at ELMs (analysis/flatTop.ts documents that bias), and those frames now sample the post-crash trough of the ELM cycle instead of the pre-crash peak, so the bias flips sign. Flat-top P_fus (Q moves alike): ITER 538.3 -> 523.4 MW (-2.8 %, Q 10.42 -> 10.13, Ti 8.28 -> 8.20 keV), DEMO 2077.6 -> 2025.1 MW (-2.5 %, Q 20.65 -> 20.13), JT60SA -2.0 %, ITER-pB11 -2.1 %, SPARC 174.7 -> 173.5 MW (-0.7 %), DIIID -0.6 %, JET 12.36 -> 12.30 MW (-0.5 %), ITER-DHe3 -0.4 %, MASTU -0.1 %. Report maxima over frames (ELM frames used to hold the pre-crash peak): Tmax_keV ITER 20.12 -> 19.89 (-1.1 %), JT60SA -1.1 %, DEMO -0.4 %, SPARC -0.2 %, DIIID -0.2 %, JET -0.1 %; Q_sci_max ITER 13.69 -> 13.40 (-2.1 %), JT60SA -1.6 %, DEMO 22.43 -> 22.20 (-1.0 %), SPARC -0.6 %, JET -0.5 %, DIIID -0.4 %; tripleProduct_max / lawson_ratio ITER -0.4 %, JT60SA -2.6 %, DEMO -0.8 %, DIIID -0.6 %; JT60SA score 35 -> 34. Derived engineering numbers follow the flat-top P_fus with leverage: ITER net P_electric -10 %, LCOE +11 %, EROI -7.7 %; DEMO net P_electric -3.7 %, LCOE +3.6 %. Largest single moves: ITER report scalars 0.108 (net electric, LCOE), traces up to 0.073 (single sampled frames at an ELM), history statistics up to 0.028. npm run validate still passes (29 passed, 6 known failures).

## 2026-09-28 22:27 UTC — ws3d (v4/ws3d on fca4643): 1.5D counterparts of the ws2b 0D physics fixes, and the transport-geometry cell-volume fix. Only the 9 1.5D cases move (ITER15, JET15, SPARC15, SPARC15-short, DEMO15, DIIID15, MASTU15, SPARC15-DHe3, SPARC15-pB11); the 21 non-1.5D goldens are untouched. The robustness commits before the physics (typed numerical failures, checkpoint contract, blank profile settings) were checked bit-identical: all 9 1.5D goldens matched after them. Causes (S, L, F, G below; per-case numbers are flat-top frame means unless noted, stage moves in brackets): (S) FusionSource and NbiSource per channel: D-D thermal rate pairDensity 0.5 (n_a + n_b)^2 (it was 0.5 n_a^2, fA^2 too low for fuelFracA < 1); burn-up and ash per channel through burnPerReaction and FuelChannel.ash (the D-D side channels of D-3He consume 2 D each and make 1/2 ash; the ash per reaction used to be one constant per fuel); the beam-target rate per channel with its own target (beamTargetDensity); the beam-target reactions of D-D were all booked on channel 0 = D(d,p)T, which has no neutron and is fully charged, so the beam-target neutron yield and the neutron share of the power were wrong; every charged product (alpha 3.5 / 3.67 MeV, protons 3.02 / 14.68, T 1.01, He3 0.82, the 3 alpha of p-11B) heats on its own Stix critical energy (it was the Echarged of channel 0 as a mass-4 ion for every fuel; the D-T alpha now has A = 4.0015 instead of 4, which changes E_c by 0.04 % and, through the chaotic ELM and sawtooth timing, the counts of ELMs and the phase of q(0) by up to 10 % in DEMO15 and JET15 with no systematic effect). (L) Loss power P_L = P_heat - P_rad,core - dW/dt for the tau_E scaling and the L-H test, as in 0D (it was P_heat - P_rad): P_rad,core is the bremsstrahlung and line radiation from rho < 0.6 plus all synchrotron radiation (ITER15 22.6 of 31.1 MW), dW/dt is the rate of change of the stored energy including the energy the ELM crashes take out, filtered with tau_E (ITER15 flat-top: 0.48 MW, against +7.2 MW continuous between the ELMs); P_LH is pLH_threshold (Martin 2008 with the Ryter 2014 low-density branch, P_LH proportional to 1/nbar below n_min): at the start-up density the threshold is higher (SPARC15 n_min 1.9e20 m^-3: P_LH 41 MW instead of 14 MW at nbar 1.0e20), so the L-H transitions come later: ITER15 6.5 -> 8.5 s, JET15 0.06 -> 0.22 s, SPARC15 0.81 -> 1.50 s, SPARC15-short 0.79 -> 1.48 s, SPARC15-pB11 0.05 -> 1.42 s, DEMO15 11.9 -> 15.0 s (DIIID15 and MASTU15 unchanged); removing the Ryter branch alone changes the SPARC15-short flat-top by 1-5 % and its E_fusion and T_max by -6.6 % and -14.5 %. In a steady H-mode P_L rises (the mantle radiation is no longer subtracted) and tau_E falls with P_L^-0.69; in a ramp the scaling relaxes W with the time constant tau_E (W' = P - W/tau_E) instead of jumping to W = tau_E P. One stored-energy definition (rounding only). (F) fast-ion pressure: beta_T and beta_N carry the pressure of the NBI ions and of the fast alpha (P tau_W of the Stix slowing-down distributions, W_alpha and W_beam), betaN_th (thermal) seeds the NTMs and beta_p stays thermal; diagnostics only (the Troyon test now sees the total beta_N). (G) cell volumes are the integral of V' over the cell (scaled once to the volume of the equilibrium) instead of V(rho) splined through the coarse table: outer cells 1.0-2.5 % (MASTU15 15 %) different, flat-top effects at most 1-2 % apart from MASTU15. Cases: ITER15: Q 9.744 -> 10.34 (+6.1 %: L +6.0 %), P_fus 490.1 -> 519.8 MW, P_alpha 97.5 -> 103.4, P_L 117.2 -> 130.5 MW, tau_E 2.554 -> 2.357 s, W 295.9 -> 304.3 MJ, T_e(0) 23.84 -> 24.62 keV, f_bs 0.2235 -> 0.2300, Q_sci_max 16.00 -> 16.23, E_fusion 208.1 -> 217.1 GJ, T_max 28.24 -> 28.69 keV, Q_eng 1.194 -> 1.251, ELMs 1020 -> 1101, sawtooth crashes 36 -> 38, GS updates 23 -> 25; beta_N 1.481 -> 1.716 (thermal 1.524; W_f 38.4 MJ, P_beam_heat 32.7 MW); score 95 -> 94. JET15: Q_sci_max 1.249 -> 0.9805 (L: -21.5 %, the ramp-up W follows the tau_E relaxation, so Q stays below 1: burn time 0.247 -> 0 s and the burn_start and burn_end events are gone), E_fusion 85.41 -> 82.35 MJ (-3.6 %), neutron yield -3.6 %, ELMs 149 -> 146, flat-top Q and P_fus within 0.1 % (14.63 -> 14.64 MW), q(0) 1.408 -> 1.400, V_loop -1.1 %, GS 15/3/1 -> 14/3/1 accepted/retried/rejected; beta_N 1.369 -> 1.626 (thermal 1.373; NBI 29.0 MW absorbed, W_f 1.72 MJ); validate JET15 E_fusion (known failure, range 40-80) 85.4 -> 82.3 MJ. SPARC15: Q 6.087 -> 6.319 (+3.8 %), P_fus 156.3 -> 162.1 MW, P_L 45.99 -> 49.02 MW, tau_E 0.5654 -> 0.5410 s, W 25.99 -> 26.52 MJ, T_e(0) 25.12 -> 25.62 keV, Q_sci_max 7.241 -> 6.684 (-7.7 %, L: start-up), E_fusion 1373 -> 1327 MJ, T_max 25.85 -> 26.76 keV, Q_eng 0.6565 -> 0.6758, ELMs 6 -> 0 (the pedestal is marginal), beta_N 1.087 -> 1.225 (thermal 1.109), q(0) -2.6 % (L) +1.4 % (G). SPARC15-short (3 s: the flat-top window is still the ramp-up): Q 6.54 -> 5.174 (-20.9 %, L), P_fus 166.8 -> 132.6 MW, P_L 46.3 -> 34.98 MW, tau_E 0.510 -> 0.616 s, W 27.8 -> 24.0 MJ, T_e(0) 33.35 -> 30.78 keV, Q_sci_max 7.298 -> 5.442, E_fusion 272.0 -> 188.7 MJ, T_max 68.13 -> 36.76 keV, neutron yield -30.6 %, score 85 -> 81. DEMO15: Q 22.74 -> 23.26 (+2.3 %), P_fus 2270 -> 2322 MW, P_L 425.0 -> 446.6 MW, tau_E 2.946 -> 2.847 s, W 1242 -> 1262 MJ, Q_sci_max 23.45 -> 23.82, E_fusion +1.8 %, ELMs 1072 -> 1095, q(0) 1.815 -> 1.645 (S -10.8 %: ELM and sawtooth phase), V_loop 16.8 -> 14.2 mV; beta_N 2.222 -> 2.804 (thermal 2.257, W_f 305 MJ, P_beam_heat 49.7 MW). DIIID15 (D-D): P_alpha 4.09e-3 -> 2.93e-3 MW (-28 %, S: the beam-target reactions are split between the charged D(d,p)T and the neutron D(d,n)3He branch), neutron yield x4.1 (2.35e15 -> 9.59e15; report 2.73e15 -> 1.12e16, S), Q_sci_max 6.33e-4 -> 5.22e-4 (L), E_fusion -1.4 %, q(0) 1.339 -> 1.398, beta_N 1.677 -> 2.132 (thermal 1.678; beam W_f 0.349 MJ, P_beam_heat 11.96 MW), GS 9/1/0 -> 9/0/0. MASTU15 (D-D, documented poor case: hollow current at beta_p about 1-2, not validated): neutron yield x53 (2.43e13 -> 1.28e15; report 2.57e13 -> 1.53e15) and P_fus +28 % (7.25e-4 -> 9.30e-4 MW), P_alpha -15 % (S, beam-target on both D-D branches); the run then takes another branch of the Grad-Shafranov retry ladder (accepted/retried/rejected 2/2/5 -> 4/1/3): q(0) 0.225 -> 2.755, q95 27.7 -> 17.6, l_i 0.657 -> 0.774, f_bs 0.319 -> 0.435, V_loop 0.0446 -> 0.0833 V, T_e(0) 2.50 -> 3.13 keV, T_i(0) 1.76 -> 3.40 keV, T_max 1.86 -> 3.69 keV, E_fusion 1.40 -> 1.76 kJ, score 22 -> 24; beta_N 2.587 -> 4.180 (thermal 2.827; W_f 0.144 MJ) with a second warning (0.85 of the Troyon limit); G moves q(0) by +16 % here. SPARC15-DHe3 (3 s): no L-H transition any more (H-mode fraction 0.274 -> 0, LH and HL events gone): at the old transition (1.3 s, P_L = P_LH = 18.6 MW) the new P_L is 15.9 MW (dW/dt of 4.4 MW is subtracted while the 25 MW of ICRH heats the ramp; the threshold is 27 MW there, Ryter branch at nbar 1.5e20 < n_min 1.9e20) and later P_L 23.07 MW stays below P_LH 26.03 MW (P_L 19.3 -> 23.1 MW at the end is the core-only radiation); in L-mode tau_E 0.599 -> 0.428 s, W 13.28 -> 9.83 MJ, T_e(0) 24.8 -> 17.3 keV, P_fus 0.417 -> 0.0970 MW, Q 0.0158 -> 0.00344, E_fusion 1.096 -> 0.283 MJ, neutron yield -50 %, T_max 47.4 -> 29.8 keV, GS 18 -> 13 accepted. SPARC15-pB11: Q +4.1 %, P_fus 1.42e-3 -> 1.47e-3 MW, P_L 9.89 -> 10.41 MW (P_rad,core 13.4 of 16.0 MW: the run is radiation dominated), tau_E 1.598 -> 1.551 s, T_e(0) 50.8 -> 44.8 keV, Q_sci_max 1.158e-4 -> 8.74e-5, E_fusion 4.01 -> 3.38 kJ, T_max 39.7 -> 35.5 keV. New keys in every 1.5D case: P_beam_heat, P_rad_core, W_alpha, W_beam, betaN_th, ignited (0 in every frame: no golden case ignites and none uses heating.autoOff), dWdt_s, and Wf and P_loss now with the new definitions; the checkpoint records gain crashE, dWdtS, tAuxOff.

## 2026-09-28 23:45 UTC — ws3d review fix: the fast-ion energy content of the 1.5D model is a pool that builds up with tau_W (dW/dt = P - W/tau_W, exact step W' = W_ss + (W - W_ss) exp(-dt/tau_W), as the 0D model) instead of the steady slowing-down content P tau_W applied at once. The content that entered beta_T and beta_N jumped to P tau_W at the first step; tau_W = tau_se (1 - G)/2 is 1 s or more at start-up densities and fast beams, so it exceeded the injected energy several times over (JET15 with a 500 keV beam: W_beam 13.6 MJ against 3.0 MJ injected at 0.44 s) and beta_N reached 3.6 at 0.5 s, where the Troyon limit ended the shot (0D ran to the end). This entry only changes the fast-ion keys and the two total-pressure keys of the nine 1.5D cases that the entry of the ws3d parity re-record (commit d0ea19b: loss power P_L = P_heat - P_rad,core - dW/dt, Ryter L-H threshold, per-channel fusion and NBI sources, ignition and autoOff, fast-ion pressure, cell volumes) left standing: everything else, every thermal and transport key, is bit-identical to that entry, and the 21 other cases are unchanged. The moves relative to the golden of d0ea19b (old -> new): - keys moved everywhere: W_alpha, W_beam, Wf (flat top and history max/mean/min: the pools start at 0 in the first frame, the old first frame already had the steady content), betaN and betaT (history extremes and means, tiny flat-top moves). Nothing else moved. - ITER15: flat-top Wf 38.359 -> 38.411 MJ (W_beam 12.814 -> 12.866, W_alpha 25.5448 -> 25.5448), betaN 1.71548 -> 1.71574; history Wf max 91.67 -> 88.72 MJ, W_beam max 37.09 -> 35.65, betaN max 2.3055 -> 2.3059. - JET15: flat-top Wf 1.7240 -> 1.7272 MJ, betaN 1.6261 -> 1.6266; history betaN max 2.4767 -> 2.3216 (-6.3 %: the start-up peak at 0.65 s, when the ramp is fastest and the pool has not yet built up; thermal beta_N there is 1.71, the Troyon limit 3.5), Wf max 5.220 -> 4.295 MJ, W_alpha max 2.113 -> 1.315, W_beam max 3.266 -> 2.998. - SPARC15: flat-top Wf 2.7808 -> 2.7815 MJ, betaN 1.22519 -> 1.22522; history Wf max 3.714 -> 3.612. SPARC15-short (3 s ramp): flat-top Wf 3.4095 -> 3.4034, betaN 1.14644 -> 1.14619; history Wf mean 1.759 -> 1.592, betaN max 1.1643 -> 1.1677. - DEMO15: flat-top Wf 305.33 -> 305.23 MJ, betaN 2.8039 -> 2.8037; history W_beam max 100.6 -> 94.7 MJ, betaN max 2.8538 -> 2.8524. - DIIID15 (D-D, 3 s): flat-top W_beam 0.34840 -> 0.34896 MJ, betaN 2.13228 -> 2.13301; history betaN max 2.577 -> 2.364 (-8.3 %), Wf max 0.786 -> 0.715 MJ. - MASTU15 (documented poor case, not validated): flat-top betaN 4.17964 -> 4.18026; history betaN max 5.265 -> 4.583 (-13 %), betaN min 0.816 -> 0.629; the 'approaching the Troyon limit' warning (0.85 x 5.5 = 4.675) is no longer issued because the start-up peak stays below it: events.warning 2 -> 1 (only the Grad-Shafranov rejection remains). - SPARC15-DHe3 (3 s ramp): flat-top W_alpha 0.00864 -> 0.01009 MJ (the pool keeps what it built at the lower start-up density, where tau_W is longer), betaN 0.41124 -> 0.41130. SPARC15-pB11: flat-top W_alpha 3.32e-4 -> 4.09e-4 MJ, betaN 0.53205 -> 0.53205. The same review round adds a step that puts y and the context scalars back when a plug-in hook throws after the implicit attempts (no golden number involved) and tests that pin the scaling-mode tau_E to the loss power.

## 2026-09-29 00:35 UTC — ws3d addendum (no golden file changed): the knock-on moves of the 22:27 and 23:45 entries that their reasons did not list (found by the ws3d review and the Wave-1 gate)

Node v24.19.0 · `npm run golden:update` was not run for this entry · numbers compare the golden files at fca4643 (before ws3d) with the golden files now (v4/integration at the Wave-1 gate); every value below is a flat-top frame mean or an event count of the case named. All of them are consequences of causes the two entries above already give (the loss power P_L = P_heat - P_rad,core - dW/dt with the Ryter L-H branch, the fast-ion pools, the cell-volume fix, the D-D beam-target split, the branch of the Grad-Shafranov retry ladder); they are listed so that no moved headline number is left without a stated cause.

- SPARC15-short (3 s, its flat-top window is still the ramp-up): T_ped 5.894 -> 5.214 keV (-11.5 %), f_bs 0.1926 -> 0.1689 (-12.3 %), q(0) 0.988 -> 0.962, l_i 0.746 -> 0.754. Cause: dW/dt in the loss power (W' = P - W/tau_E instead of W = tau_E P), which is what moves this case's Q 6.54 -> 5.17 and its stored energy; the ELM count 6 -> 0 has the same cause (the L-H transition moves from 0.79 to 1.48 s).
- SPARC15-DHe3: T_ped 2.088 -> 1.120 keV (-46 %), f_bs 0.0957 -> 0.0717 (-25 %), beta_N 0.555 -> 0.411, GS updates accepted 18 -> 13. Cause: the case loses its H-mode (P_L = 23.1 MW stays below P_LH = 26.0 MW; the LH and HL events are gone), so there is no pedestal.
- SPARC15-pB11: T_ped 2.152 -> 2.440 keV (+13 %), f_bs -0.6 %, q(0) 0.941 -> 0.925. Cause: P_L (radiation dominated: 9.89 -> 10.41 MW) and the L-H transition at 1.42 s instead of 0.05 s (Ryter branch); the pedestal is read at a different phase of the ramp-up.
- SPARC15: T_ped 4.755 -> 4.845 keV (+1.9 %), f_bs +1.6 %, ELMs 6 -> 0 (the L-H transition moves from 0.81 to 1.50 s, Ryter branch, which shortens the H-mode window).
- MASTU15 (documented poor case): T_ped 0.1465 -> 0.1189 keV (-19 %), ELMs 213 -> 200, f_bs 0.319 -> 0.435, l_i 0.657 -> 0.774, q95 27.7 -> 17.6, q(0) 0.225 -> 2.755. Cause: the D-D beam-target neutron correction (neutron yield x59 and P_fus +28 % against fca4643) and then a different branch of the GS retry ladder (accepted/retried/rejected 2/2/5 -> 4/1/3): the q profile and the heating depend on which updates are accepted.
- ITER15: ELMs 1020 -> 1101, sawtooth crashes 36 -> 38, GS updates 23 -> 25, f_bs 0.2235 -> 0.2300 (+2.9 %), T_ped 3.716 -> 3.739 keV. Cause: the higher loss power (P_L 117 -> 130 MW) and the later L-H transition (6.5 -> 8.5 s); the counts follow the ELM and sawtooth cycle phase.
- DEMO15: ELMs 1072 -> 1095, GS updates 30 -> 31, f_bs 0.4221 -> 0.4283 (+1.5 %), q(0) 1.815 -> 1.645 (ELM-cycle phase, as the ws3d review noted). JET15: ELMs 149 -> 146, GS updates 15 -> 14 (14 accepted, 3 of them after a retry, 1 rejected). DIIID15: ELMs 250 -> 251, T_ped -0.3 %, q(0) 1.339 -> 1.398.
- No golden case has an ignited frame in either fidelity (`ignited` is 0 in every frame of every case), so the golden files do not guard the ignition path; src/physics/regress/fastIonPools.test.ts, autoOff.test.ts, ignitionQuench.test.ts and src/physics/profiles/ignition.test.ts do.

## 2026-09-29 01:29 UTC — ws5s: frames recorded at an L-H flip of the 0D magnetic model now carry the diagnostics of their own (post-flip) state; before, the frame stored the post-flip H_mode next to the tau_E, P_cond and P_ELM cached from the step's last rhs(), which was evaluated before postStep flipped the mode. The run itself is bitwise unchanged (same states, events, report numbers, ELM and sawtooth counts, flat-top windows); only single frames move, and therefore the frame statistics (history mean and min) of the diagnostics that depend on the confinement mode.

Cause. MagneticModel.postStep sets hmode (L-H, H-L) or ntm (sawtooth-seeded NTM onset, decay) after the step, but diagnostics() returned the pre-flip lastDiag. postStep now marks the cache stale at an ELM crash and at every flip and calls refreshDiagnostics() once, before the burn logic and before a disruption or the scheduled end can change the phase. rhs() overwrites the cache at the next step and refreshDiagnostics() restores tau_E for the sawtooth timing, so nothing but the frame changes. Only L-H frames are affected here (each of the five cases below has one flip that ends on a recorded frame; the JT60SA, DEMO, ITER-DHe3 and ITER-pB11 flips end a step that is not a recorded frame, and the 1.5D cases need no refresh: their frames carry the diagnostics the step wrote together with its own H_mode).

The single frames that changed (measured on the golden configurations against the same run without the refresh; tau_E [s], P_cond and P_ELM [MW]): - ITER, L-H at 9.867 s: tau_E 2.1222 -> 4.4319, P_cond 37.972 -> 12.728, P_ELM 0 -> 5.4549. - JET, L-H at 0.345 s: tau_E 0.29177 -> 0.38442, P_cond 7.5620 -> 4.0176, P_ELM 0 -> 1.7218. - SPARC, L-H at 1.440 s: tau_E 0.43058 -> 0.79728, P_cond 22.242 -> 8.4084, P_ELM 0 -> 3.6036. - DIIID, L-H at 0.112 s: tau_E 0.13378 -> 0.18600, P_cond 2.0809 -> 1.0477, P_ELM 0 -> 0.44900. - MASTU, L-H at 0.050 s: tau_E 0.055627 -> 0.11878, P_cond 1.2159 -> 0.39858, P_ELM 0 -> 0.17082.

Headline moves (old -> new, relative), all frame statistics over the whole history: tau_E.mean ITER 2.71454 -> 2.71535 (+3.0e-4), JET 0.283502 -> 0.283564 (+2.2e-4), SPARC 0.571478 -> 0.571723 (+4.3e-4), DIIID 0.0870067 -> 0.0870415 (+4.0e-4), MASTU 0.062782 -> 0.062845 (+1.0e-3); tau_E.min MASTU 0.055627 -> 0.056334 (+1.3 %, the L-H frame was the minimum). P_cond.mean ITER -1.1e-4, JET -1.1e-4, SPARC -3.0e-4, DIIID -5.8e-5, MASTU -2.0e-4; P_cond.min DIIID 1.0866 -> 1.0477 (-3.6 %), MASTU 0.40341 -> 0.39858 (-1.2 %). P_ELM.mean ITER +5.6e-5, JET +1.2e-4, SPARC +1.9e-4, DIIID +5.9e-5, MASTU +9.8e-5. P_transport.mean -2.3e-5 to -1.6e-4 in all five, P_transport.min MASTU 0.57629 -> 0.56941 (-1.2 %). lawson.mean and triple.mean +5.2e-5 (JET) to +1.8e-4 (SPARC; MASTU +1.7e-4), lawson.min and triple.min MASTU +2.7 %.

Nothing else moved: flatTop.*, the report scalars (Q, P_fus, E_fus, T_max, ...), the events and every other history key are bit-identical, and the other 25 cases are unchanged (JT60SA, DEMO, W7X, all nine 1.5D cases, the pulsed and concept cases). Guarded by src/physics/kernel/modeFlipFrames.test.ts (a frame's diagnostics equal a fresh evaluation from its own state at the L-H frames of DIII-D, JET and ITER, an H-L frame, and NTM onset and decay frames; they fail on the previous code).

## 2026-09-29 01:44 UTC — ws3s stage A: the radial grid of the 1.5D model is edge-packed by default (ProfileSettings.gridPacking 4: a tanh step in the cell density centred at 1 - 1.25 w_ped, width 0.75 w_ped): at nRho 50 the edge cells are 5 times narrower (0.0054 against 0.02), the core cells 30 % wider (0.026) and 10 cells lie across the pedestal (3 before). D4 convergence of ITER15 (flat-top means): T_ped 5.12 / 3.74 / 3.61 keV at nRho 25 / 50 / 100 with the uniform grid, 3.56 / 3.37 / 3.36 keV with the packed grid; the change between nRho 50 and 100 of Q, f_bs, l_i and T_ped falls from 1.6 / 0.1 / 1.7 / 3.4 % to 0.1 / 0.1 / 0.2 / 0.3 %. Every finite-volume solver, stencil and lookup uses per-cell and per-face spacings (gridPacking 0 reproduces the previous results bit for bit). Two definitions change with the packed grid and only with it: T_ped is the electron temperature at rho_ped interpolated between the two cell centres around it (the uniform grid took the cell that contains rho_ped, 0.02 wide in the steepest gradient of the barrier), and the alpha check of the ELM trigger includes the separatrix face (half a cell, where the barrier gradient is steepest). The nine 1.5D cases move (the twenty-one others are unchanged); flat-top means (frame-weighted, the golden definition), uniform grid to packed grid: ITER15 Q 10.34 to 10.54 (+2.0 %), P_fus 519.8 to 530.1 MW, f_bs 0.2300 to 0.2298, l_i 0.7243 to 0.7381 (+1.9 %), T_ped 3.739 to 3.375 keV (-9.7 %), q95 3.479 to 3.456, alpha_ped/alpha_crit 0.777 to 0.819, ELMs 1101 to 1131, sawtooth crashes 38 to 25 and q(0) 1.031 to 1.107 (both follow the core resolution: 9 and 7 crashes at nRho 100 and 200); JET15 Q 0.4413 to 0.4448, T_ped 1.679 to 1.575 keV (-6.2 %), q(0) 1.400 to 1.493, 15 GS updates accepted (14) and 1 rejected; SPARC15 Q 6.319 to 6.353, T_ped 4.845 to 4.806 keV, alpha_ped 0.827 to 0.977 and 14 ELMs where there were none (the pedestal gradient of the last cells now reaches the ELM limit), q(0) 0.899 to 0.858; SPARC15-short Q 5.174 to 5.161, T_ped 5.214 to 5.133 keV; DEMO15 Q 23.26 to 23.36, T_ped 4.198 to 3.773 keV (-10.1 %), q(0) 1.645 to 1.442, ELMs 1095 to 1109; SPARC15-DHe3 Q 3.44e-3 to 3.40e-3, T_ped 1.120 to 0.995 keV; SPARC15-pB11 Q 5.38e-5 to 5.37e-5, T_ped 2.440 to 2.316 keV, q(0) 0.924 to 1.064; DIIID15 Q 2.954e-4 to 2.984e-4, T_ped 0.630 to 0.560 keV (-11.1 %), q(0) 1.398 to 1.264; MASTU15 (documented poor case, do not quote) Q 1.95e-4 to 1.98e-4, T_ped 0.119 to 0.115 keV, q(0) 2.75 to 2.14, GS updates accepted 4 to 7 and rejected 3 to 4. The Grad-Shafranov updates need more retries with the steeper pedestal pressure the packed grid resolves (DEMO15 2 rejected and 11 retried of 32, DIIID15 1 rejected and 5 retried of 10). The steps grow by 1-10 % (ITER15 10408 to 10959, SPARC15 835 to 923). The equilibrium digests, every radial profile and every frame of the nine cases move with the grid.

## 2026-09-29 01:46 UTC — ws7b systems-lite engineering: the engineering block of the magnetic shot report is re-derived (TF coil stress, magnetic energy, TBR, recirculating power) and extended with the TF coil, CS flux, cryoplant, radial-build keys; no plasma quantity moved (history, flat-top means, traces, events, geometry, profiles and the equilibrium digest of every case are unchanged, and the ICF, MTF, FRC, mirror and muon cases do not change at all)

What moved and why. (1) TF stress (MPa): the thin-ring estimate 0.5 (B^2 / 2 mu0) R_coil / t (82 MPa for ITER, which neglected the vertical tension and the load sharing of case and winding pack) is replaced by the Tresca stress of the PROCESS-style two-layer plane-stress analysis of the inboard leg (Kovari et al., Fusion Eng. Des. 104 (2016) 9, sect. 2.4; PROCESS sctfcoil; a nose case, a smeared winding pack carrying the Lorentz body force and the plasma-side case, plus the vertical tension of the coil divided by the steel area). The layer solver and the vertical force reproduce the reference values of the PROCESS unit tests to better than 1e-8. ITER 82 -> 489 MPa (limit 660), JET 27 -> 143, SPARC 197 -> 1024 (over its 800 MPa limit: one more warning; the 0.5 m leg is thin for 22 T), DIII-D 13 -> 73, JT-60SA 14 -> 97, MAST-U 3 -> 29, W7-X 29 -> 120 (50 modular coils, an estimate), DEMO 133 -> 745 (over 660 MPa: one more warning; the same model gives 571 MPa for the 1.4 m leg of the PROCESS DEMO build). (2) Magnetic energy (GJ): the cavity integral (pi h R^2 B0^2 / mu0) ln(R_out / R_in) with the D-shaped coil height replaces the rough volume estimate: ITER 37.7 -> 39.6 (41 GJ built), 1.5D ITER15 37.7 -> 41.8 (equilibrium elongation), DEMO 141.4 -> 149.0, JET 1.69 -> 1.81, W7-X 0.46 -> 0.73; it enters the copper-coil resistance term of the recirculating power (JET 471 -> 495 MW, DIII-D 94 -> 92). (3) TBR: the fit of Shimwell et al., Fusion Eng. Des. 104 (2016) 34 (its Table 3; the transcription reproduces its Table 4 maxima 1.247, 1.261, 1.264) at the 6Li enrichment and the mean depth of the blanket of the new radial build, scaled with the coverage relative to the model (0.9): ITER, ITER15, ITER-DHe3, ITER-pB11 1.199 -> 1.125, DEMO and DEMO15 1.199 -> 1.143. (4) Recirculating power: the cryoplant power of the superconducting coils, computed from the heat load (static 4.3e-4 W/kg and 2 W/m^2, TF nuclear heating from the PROCESS fit, pulsed-field losses, current leads, 45 % margin; the Slack model of PROCESS, reproduced to better than 1e-9 against its unit test) times cryo_W_per_W of the technology, replaces the flat 5 MW. ITER 5 -> 34.7 MW (P_recirculating 181 -> 211, net electric 43 -> 13 MW, Q_eng 1.237 -> 1.063, LCOE 2478 -> 8043 $/MWh, EROI 1.2 -> 1.1), ITER15 177 -> 208 (net 44 -> 14, LCOE 2402 -> 7687), DEMO 378 -> 409 (net 524 -> 493, LCOE 667 -> 709, Q_eng 2.385 -> 2.205, EROI 2.4 -> 2.2), DEMO15 391 -> 428 (net 637 -> 601, LCOE 559 -> 593), SPARC 100 -> 120 (a cryoplant of 25 MW at 20 K from a TF nuclear heating of 419 kW of the PROCESS fit, which is extrapolated to a build without a blanket: the largest single doubt of this change), W7-X 44 -> 47, ITER-pB11 212 -> 277. The neutron wall load, dpa per year, divertor peak flux, fusion power and thermal power are unchanged; the Capital cost (M$) is unchanged (the Sheffield-Milora volume scaling was kept).

New report keys (added to every magnetic case): TF coils, TF winding pack J (MA/m2), TF case Tresca (MPa), TF winding pack Tresca (MPa), TF stress margin, TF vertical tension per coil (MN), TF mass (t); CS flux swing (V s), Flux required (V s), Flux margin (tokamaks; the required flux is the inductive plus Ejima resistive plus burn flux, the capability that of a solenoid of thickness B / mu0 J); TF nuclear heating (kW), Cryo heat load (kW), Cryoplant power (MW) (superconducting coils); Inboard blanket (m), Inboard shield+vessel (m) (with a blanket). The number of warnings moves where the TF stress exceeds its limit (SPARC and its variants +1, DEMO and DEMO15 1 -> 2). Reproduce with npm run golden.

## 2026-09-29 01:59 UTC — ws7a (edge physics): edge channels added to the magnetic cases; the 1.5D P_SOL is the ELM-inclusive balance

1. Keys added, no value moved (all nine magnetic 0D cases: ITER, JET, SPARC, DIIID, JT60SA, MASTU, DEMO, ITER-DHe3, ITER-pB11; the ledger below shows "0 keys moved" for them): the edge diagnostics of src/physics/edge, ten channels in every frame (P_sep_R, lambda_q, T_u, T_t, q_peak, f_pwr, detach, cz_det, q_det, p_div; their history statistics and flat-top means) and four numeric entries of the report's engineering table (P_sep/R, target T_e, target q_peak, c_z for detachment; the detachment state is text and not in the snapshot). Stellarators, ICF, MTF, Z-pinch, FRC, mirror and muon cases have no such channels and are unchanged.

2. Values moved (the nine 1.5D cases, on top of the added keys), one cause: acceptStep updates the lagged P_SOL after the smoothed dW/dt (ctx.dWdtS, which includes the energy the ELM crashes took out of the plasma) and passes that instead of the step-wise dW/dt. P_SOL is now the ELM-averaged power across the separatrix P_heat - P_rad - dW/dt, the same dW/dt as in the loss power of the confinement time. It feeds q_div (divertor heat flux), the legacy conduction-limited T_sep (proportional to P_SOL^(2/7)) and, for a tungsten impurity, the W source. Flat-top means, old -> new: ITER15 P_SOL 110.3 -> 121.8 MW (+10.5 %; the ELM-inclusive balance of the report is 122.0), q_div 26.0 -> 28.7 MW/m2, T_sep 0.370 -> 0.384 keV (+3.8 %); JET15 P_SOL 30.2 -> 35.7 MW (+18.3 %), q_div 7.09 -> 8.39, T_sep 0.228 -> 0.240 keV; DEMO15 P_SOL 411.3 -> 431.6 MW (+4.9 %, the balance is 431.2), q_div 51.4 -> 54.0, T_sep stays at its 0.5 keV clamp; DIIID15 P_SOL 13.9 -> 14.9 MW (+7.6 %), T_sep 0.170 -> 0.173 keV; MASTU15 +0.2 %; SPARC15 unchanged (no ELMs, P_heat - P_rad - dW/dt = the old value); SPARC15-short (3 s, still in the ramp-up) P_SOL 38.8 -> 33.2 MW (-14.3 %) and SPARC15-pB11 10.3 -> 7.9 MW (-23.5 %), SPARC15-DHe3 -2.4 %: without ELMs the smoothed dW/dt lags the ramp by tau_E, so P_SOL of a ramp-up window follows it with that lag. The changed T_sep moves the boundary cell and with it the run at the 1e-3 level: ITER15 Q 10.34 -> 10.33, P_fus 519.8 -> 519.3 MW, T_ped 3.739 -> 3.754 keV, beta_N 1.716 -> 1.715, tau_E 2.357 s unchanged; JET15 Q 0.4413 -> 0.4402; DEMO15 Q 23.26 -> 23.31, q(0) 1.645 -> 1.621; DIIID15 T_ped 0.630 -> 0.636 keV. The thousands of moved profile keys (largest relative changes in shear, Pohm, johm, chie at single cells and in the axis position Zaxis) are the phase of the ELM and sawtooth sequence, which follows the boundary: ELM count ITER15 1101 -> 1113, DEMO15 1095 -> 1100, sawtooth crashes ITER15 38 -> 37; the other counts are unchanged.

3. Not changed by this entry: the 'twoPoint' boundary (ProfileSettings.edgeModel, off by default) and the POPCON edge maps (opt-in) touch no case of this suite.

## 2026-09-29 02:01 UTC — ws2c numbers hygiene before the Wave-3 reconciliation: time-weighted flat tops, exact D-T energies, Miller volume and surface, ITER/DEMO density targets, MAST-U scenario, Ryter branch exponent

Five intended changes, in commit order; the moves of each were measured on its own commit against the golden snapshot of the commit before it, and this last commit of the lane re-records. All numbers are flat-top means (time-weighted since change 1) unless stated. 23 of the 30 cases move; MIRROR-DHe3, MUON, TAE, TAE-pB11, W7X, Z and ZAP are unchanged to 1e-9 (pulsed and concept models with their own energies, or a steady D-D stellarator without ELM frames; W7X, the case with a fuelling-loss risk, does not reach the new density-collapse termination).

1. Flat-top weighting (76beb92). flatTopMean/flatTopAverages default to the trapezoidal time average of the last 30 % of the shot instead of the mean over the frames of that window; the extra frames at ELM and sawtooth crashes weighted the crash states. The report, the golden flat-top, the validation table and the figures use it. Moves flatTop.* and the report scalars built from it, no simulated state: 22 of 30 cases show a moved key, largest 2.2 %. ITER Q 10.130 -> 10.134, DEMO Q 20.13 -> 20.11, SPARC Q 6.590 -> 6.590, ITER15 Q 10.341 -> 10.341, GF and FRXL P_fus -2.0 %, ITER-pB11 Q +2.2 %, NIF P_fus +0.3 %, DIIID15 E_fus -0.8 %.

2. D + T energies (4ead482). 4He 3.561 MeV and neutron 14.028 MeV (exact two-body kinematics of Q = 17.589 MeV, AME2020 masses) instead of 3.5 + 14.1 MeV = 17.6 MeV: the alpha heating per reaction was 1.7 % low and P_charged + P_neutron was 1.000625 P_fus. Moves every D-T case through the alpha heating: ITER Q 10.134 -> 10.55 (+4.1 %, P_fus 523.6 -> 544.6 MW, T_i(0) +2 %), DEMO Q 20.11 -> 20.36, SPARC Q 6.59 -> 6.63, ITER15 Q 10.34 -> 10.41, DEMO15 Q 23.27 -> 23.43, JET P_alpha +1.8 %, P_alpha/P_fus 0.199 -> 0.203. ELM counts follow (ITER 1310 -> 1348). The D-T MIRROR has P_alpha +1.8 % (0.001771 -> 0.001802 MW). Cases without D-T fuel and the pulsed models are unchanged.

3. Miller volume, surface and cross-section (0e9e679, 74aa931). plasmaVolume, plasmaSurface and crossSectionArea are the exact integrals of the Miller boundary of (kappa, delta); the ellipse formulas overestimated the volume by 3.6 % (JET) to 14.3 % (MAST-U) and the surface by 4 to 18 %. ITER and DEMO carry their LCFS shape (kappa 1.85, delta 0.49 and 0.5) in profiles.lcfsKappa/lcfsDelta, which sets their 0D volume, surface and cross-section (ITER 842 m3 and 683 m2 against the design 837 m3 and 678 m2); the other presets use kappa, delta as the boundary, as the 1.5D model always did. Volume falls 3.5 % (JET), 8 % (SPARC), 9 to 10 % (DIII-D, JT-60SA) at the same inputs: SPARC Q 6.63 -> 7.54, JET Q 0.369 -> 0.387 and E_fus 64.2 -> 67.2 MJ, JT-60SA Q +12 %, DIII-D Q +11 %, ITER Q 10.55 -> 9.97 (V +1.2 %, P_fus -5.7 %, T_i -3.7 %), DEMO Q 20.36 -> 19.94, ITER-DHe3 P_fus 0.139 -> 0.112 MW, ITER-pB11 P_fus +6 %. The nine 1.5D cases keep their state (the 1.5D model builds its own volumes) and move only in the neutron wall load, the surface of which is the Miller one: ITER15 0.50 -> 0.53 MW/m2 (dpa/year 3.0 -> 3.2, neutron fluence +6.6 %), SPARC15 1.74 -> 1.86, DEMO15 1.04 -> 1.11. The 0D shot report and disruption report take the boundary shape like the 1.5D ones do: ITER report wall load 0.53 -> 0.52 MW/m2, DEMO 0.99 -> 0.87 (with the volume change SPARC 1.86 -> 2.26).

4. Low-density L-H branch (c202c5a). The threshold below Ryter's n_min rises as (n_min/n)^2 (the SPARC-study penalty, Hughes et al. 2020, as implemented in cfspopcon) instead of n_min/n; n_min itself (Ryter et al. 2014, eq. 3) is unchanged. The first L-H transition of a ramp-up moves by 0.02 to 0.3 s: JET 0.34 -> 0.44 s, JET15 0.22 -> 0.32 s, SPARC15 1.50 -> 1.57 s, DEMO15 14.55 -> 14.85 s. Effects: JET15 E_fus 82.2 -> 81.6 MJ, JET Q_sci_max 0.475 -> 0.481, SPARC15-pB11 Q 5.4e-5 -> 4.6e-5 (a 3 s run: the H-mode window is shorter), event counts of the ELM presets within 1.5 %. The ITPA20 and ITPA20-IL scalings (aad6293) are opt-in: this snapshot pair is identical to 1e-12.

5. Presets (4d62b86). ITER 0D n_target 1.0e20 -> 0.914e20 volume average (line-averaged n/n_G 0.915 -> 0.837; design 0.85: Casper et al. 2014, Shimada et al. 2007), DEMO 0D 0.75e20 -> 0.711e20 (n/n_G 1.238 -> 1.171; 2018 baseline 1.2: Siccinio et al. 2022); the 1.5D presets already regulate the line average and keep 1.0e20 and 0.75e20 (ITER15 and DEMO15 do not move). ITER Q 9.97 -> 10.04 (P_fus 513.8 -> 513.7 MW, T_i 8.06 -> 8.84 keV, tau_E 2.72 -> 2.59 s, L-H 10.0 -> 9.4 s), DEMO P_fus 2005 -> 1836 MW and Q 19.94 -> 18.27, ITER-DHe3 P_fus 0.112 -> 0.238 MW (n/n_G 0.95 -> 0.87). ITER-pB11, a marginal power balance (the Wave-1 report: 1.0e20 collapses at 28 s, 0.85e20 survives), now survives to the scheduled end: termination 'Radiative collapse (P_rad > P_heat)' -> 'Scheduled end', 152 ELMs instead of 33, 2 L-H transitions, no disruption, Q 1.6e-7 -> 2.4e-12; the golden suite has no disrupting case any more. MAST-U becomes the first-campaign scenario (R 0.8 m, a 0.5 m, kappa 2.1, delta 0.47, 0.75 MA, 0.55 T, 2 MW NBI absorbed, instead of the design-maximum R 0.85, a 0.65, kappa 2.5, 1 MA, 0.75 T, 5 MW): q95 18.18 -> 6.39 (published 5 to 10), P_fus 6.5e-4 -> 1.4e-4 MW, beta_N 4.77 -> 3.96, W 0.38 -> 0.12 MJ, ELMs 28 -> 11; MASTU15 (same preset, 1.5D) q95 17.54 -> 6.42, beta_N 4.18 -> 3.64, P_fus 9.3e-4 -> 2.3e-4 MW, ELMs 200 -> 297.

No golden number moves with the density-collapse termination (4ccc225: no golden case is near it), the p-11B beam-target quadrature (168b9b9: neither model calls it for p-11B) or the test-only commits. Net change of the headline numbers against the golden of the Wave-1 gate: ITER Q 10.13 -> 10.04, P_fus 523.4 -> 513.7 MW, E_fus 207 -> 202 GJ, n/n_G 0.915 -> 0.837; SPARC Q 6.59 -> 7.56, JET Q 0.369 -> 0.386, JT-60SA Q +12 %, DIII-D Q +11 %, DEMO Q 20.13 -> 18.27; ITER15 Q 10.34 -> 10.41, DEMO15 Q 23.26 -> 23.46, JET15 Q 0.4413 -> 0.4413, SPARC15 Q 6.319 -> 6.345.

## 2026-09-29 06:35 UTC — ws4i: edge-clustered 101-node flux-surface table and self-consistent Grad-Shafranov updates move the nine 1.5D cases (ITER15, JET15, SPARC15, SPARC15-short, DEMO15, SPARC15-DHe3, SPARC15-pB11, DIIID15, MASTU15); the 21 other cases (0D, fixed-fuel, direct, mirror, muon...) are bit-identical.

Cause 1, ee239b3 (surface table). The default output table of the Grad-Shafranov solver was 51 nodes at psi_N = (k/50)^2, whose last interval spans 0.04 of psi_N where q, <|grad psi|^2> and dV/dpsi_N follow a boundary layer of about 1e-3. It is now 101 nodes at psi_N = s + s^2 - s^3, s = (k/100)^2 (slope 0 at the LCFS). The outer-face metrics (g1, g2, V', q, rho_tor of the outer nodes) are within 0.5 % of a 401-surface table (measured <= 0.1 %); before they were up to 14.7 % (MASTU15) and 1.2 % (ITER15) off. At fixed shape and beta_p q95 read from the table moves ITER 3.278 -> 3.298, SPARC 3.907 -> 3.952, MASTU 16.91 -> 18.40 (801-surface references 3.298, 3.952, 18.403); l_i and beta_p do not depend on the table. On its own this commit broke the stale-table update (JET15 5 of 17 updates accepted, DEMO15 16 of 30, MASTU15 2 of 8), which is what cause 2 repairs.

Cause 2, 7673e8d (self-consistent updates). The update mapped p(rho) and <j_phi/R>(rho) to psi_N through the previous equilibrium (stale), solved once and accepted or rejected the result through a retry ladder. It is now an outer iteration (profiles/coupling/outer.ts): the table nodes are the transport radii mapped to psi_N, the values are the transport values, and the nodes move to where the new equilibrium puts the radii (under-relaxed, stagnation detected) until rho_tor(psi_N) of the equilibrium agrees with the map to 2e-3 rms; each solve is a continuation from the previous equilibrium. The core inside two grid spacings carries a flat current table. The retry ladder and binomialSmooth are retired; the catch-all around geometryFromEquilibrium is narrowed to SingularMatrixError. Anderson restartGrowth = 3 in the update solves (177 of 192 recorded table solves converge, 164 with the old rule).

GS updates accepted / retried / rejected: JET15 14 / 3 / 1 -> 15 / 1 / 0; MASTU15 4 / 1 / 3 -> 8 / 0 / 0 (hollow current at high beta_p: solved, no silent skips); ITER15 25 -> 24 and DEMO15 31 -> 30 accepted (one update fewer, the update triggers follow the trajectory); the others unchanged, none rejected.

Headline moves (whole-run scalars; last Grad-Shafranov equilibrium): ITER15 Q_sci_avg 10.86 -> 10.72 (-1.3 %), E_fusion -1.3 %, Ti max 12.08 -> 12.02 keV, q95(GS) 3.425 -> 3.538, l_i(GS) 0.7507 -> 0.7047, V_loop 0.064 -> 0.060 V, Shafranov shift 0.171 -> 0.177 m. JET15 Q_sci_avg 0.4721 -> 0.4711 (-0.2 %), q95(GS) 3.671 -> 3.756, l_i(GS) 0.7693 -> 0.7423; final T_ped 1.29 -> 1.79 keV (a sample of a run with 146 ELMs, the timing of the last ELM). SPARC15 Q_sci_avg 5.474 -> 5.456 (-0.3 %), q95(GS) 4.085 -> 4.202, l_i(GS) 0.7662 -> 0.7343. SPARC15-short Q_sci_avg 3.002 -> 3.010 (+0.3 %), q95(GS) 4.085 -> 4.213, l_i(GS) 0.7768 -> 0.7445, V_loop 0.036 -> 0.031 V. DEMO15 Q_sci_avg 21.54 -> 21.60 (+0.3 %), q95(GS) 4.850 -> 5.030, l_i(GS) 0.7736 -> 0.7216, V_loop 0.014 -> 0.011 V, Shafranov shift 0.438 -> 0.457 m. SPARC15-DHe3 Q_sci_avg -0.3 %, q95(GS) 3.903 -> 3.934, l_i(GS) 0.7983 -> 0.7932; SPARC15-pB11 Q_sci_avg -0.4 %, q95(GS) 3.912 -> 3.943, l_i(GS) 0.8138 -> 0.8083. DIIID15 Q_sci_avg -0.3 %, q95(GS) 5.825 -> 5.930, l_i(GS) 0.8369 -> 0.8148. MASTU15 Q_sci_avg 1.799e-4 -> 1.748e-4 (-2.8 %), Ti max 1.406 -> 1.351 keV, q95(GS) 14.95 -> 17.15, l_i(GS) 0.8366 -> 0.8017, l_i(3) avg 0.774 -> 0.799, warnings 3 -> 1.

The equilibrium of the last update is now consistent with the transport's own profiles: q95(GS) / q95(transport, flat-top mean) was 0.985 (ITER15), 0.995 (JET15), 0.980 (SPARC15), 0.984 (DEMO15), 0.985 (DIIID15) and 0.851 (MASTU15) and is now 1.009, 1.014, 1.000, 1.009, 0.997 and 0.984; l_i(GS) against the transport l_i was up to +8 % and is within 3 %. q95(GS) rises 1-4 % (0.8 % for the low-beta_p SPARC15 variants, 15 % for MASTU15, whose old table was 14.7 % off) with the surface table (the table read q95 too low) and l_i(GS) falls 3-7 % with the consistent mapping of the edge current. The remaining flat-top scalars (P_SOL, T_ped, V_loop, q0, dW/dt) follow within 1-6 %; the noisy ones (T_ped of the last frame, dW/dt of a flat-top mean, ELM counts) move with the timing of the ELM/sawtooth events.

## 2026-09-29 06:48 UTC — ws7a review fix: the c_z-for-detachment quantity of the edge model (channel cz_det and the report row 'c_z for detachment, Lengyel upper bound (%)'). No physics number moved: every key other than cz_det and that row is unchanged at 1e-9. Cause 1: a run of the prescribed divertor radiation that is already at or below the detachment temperature (T_t <= detachTt_eV = 5 eV) now has cz_det = 0 (it needs no seed) instead of the requirement of the unradiated tube. MASTU: flatTop.cz_det 0.804 -> 0, history.cz_det mean 0.839 -> 0.130, min 0.424 -> 0, report row 80.39 -> 0. MASTU15: flatTop.cz_det 0.507 -> 0, history.cz_det max 1 -> 0, mean 0.477 -> 0, report row 50.73 -> 0. ITER-DHe3: flatTop.cz_det 0.0244 -> 0.00097, history mean 0.0854 -> 0.0684, min 0.0089 -> 0, report row 2.44 -> 0.1. ITER-pB11: flatTop.cz_det 0.0518 -> 0.0479, history mean 0.1971 -> 0.1959. DIIID15: history mean 0.9976 -> 0.9934 (a few frames of the history are detached). Same cause in the 0D and the 1.5D cases (MASTU, MASTU15). Cause 2: the report row prints 'n/a (> 100 %)' where the model finds no attainable seeding (every flat-top frame at the cap of 1) instead of a clipped 100, and '>= X (> 100 % in N % of the flat top)' where only part of the flat top is capped. A text row is not recorded by the harness, so the numeric key disappears: JET, SPARC, DIIID, JT60SA, JET15, SPARC15, SPARC15-short, SPARC15-DHe3, SPARC15-pB11, DIIID15 (100 -> text 'n/a (> 100 %)'), ITER-pB11 (5.18 -> text, 4 of 137 flat-top frames capped; the flat top swings between detached and attached frames). The channel cz_det itself stays capped at 1.

## 2026-09-29 08:01 UTC — merge of v4/ws2c (numbers hygiene) onto v4/integration: only ITER-pB11 is re-recorded (its density goes back to 1.0e20 so that the suite keeps a disrupting shot); the other 29 cases match the ws2c re-record bit for bit; the causes of three ws2c moves that the ws2c entry did not name are given (DIIID15, MASTU15, ITER-DHe3)

1. ITER-pB11 (the only file that changes). The ws2c entry above says 'the golden suite has no disrupting case any more': after the ITER density re-base (n_target 1.0e20 -> 0.914e20, ws2c change 5) the p-11B shot ran to the scheduled end, so no golden case had a thermal quench, a current quench, the termination scalars (termination.disruption.*: t_onset, tau_TQ, tau_CQ, halo current, runaway current, wall energy density) or a non-'Scheduled end' termination label; the disruption path of the 0D model was unguarded, and the surviving case was a marginal balance (P_rad/P_heat up to 0.93 at 0.914e20) that a later radiation, density-controller or loss-power change can flip with a large unrelated diff. The case is now ITER with p-11B at the density the preset had before ws2c, 1.0e20 m^-3, as an explicit override of the case (GoldenCase.overrides.n_target; the ITER preset itself keeps 0.914e20). Against the golden of the ws2c re-record (0.914e20, 100 s, no disruption): the shot ends in a radiative-collapse disruption, 'Radiative collapse (P_rad > P_heat)': onset 27.20 s (P_rad 81.3 MW > P_heat 81.3 MW, T_e 1.98 keV), thermal quench 1.7 ms, current quench 90 ms, halo I_h/I_p TPF 0.39, runaway avalanche e^37.5 -> 10.5 MA, wall energy density 1.66 MJ/m2, W_th 51.8 MJ, W_mag 1142 MJ, melt risk 1; the shot ends at t = 27.57 s (444 frames, 1318 steps) instead of 100 s. Event counts: disruption 0 -> 1, quench 0 -> 1, ELM 152 -> 33, sawtooth 27 -> 6, LH 2 -> 1, HL 1 -> 0; every flat-top value belongs to the last 30 % of the shortened shot (P_fus 2.3e-10 -> 9.5e-6 MW, P_heat 95.9 -> 74.0 MW, P_rad 88.9 -> 59.6 MW: not comparable, the plasma is being radiated away at the end); 385 keys moved, 15 added and 1 removed (the summary below). It is the premise of the golden case of the Wave-1 gate (radiative collapse at 28.2 s there, 27.2 s now). regress/pb11Collapse.test.ts and regress/robustness.test.ts pin both densities (1.0e20 collapses at 25-32 s, 0.914e20 and 0.85e20 survive), and a test in src/regression/golden.test.ts requires the stored suite to keep a case with a disruption, a quench, the termination scalars and a non-scheduled end. No other file of test/golden changes: the 29 other cases match the ws2c re-record at 1e-9 on the merged tree (with the ws9b, ws10a, ws10w, ws10d, ws7c, ws8 and ws10b lanes, none of which moves a physics number).

2. Which ws2c commit moved three things that the ws2c entry does not name (found by the ws2c review; each case was run on the commits of the ws2c lane and bisected, c202c5a, 0e9e679 and 4d62b86 being single commits of it). - ITER-DHe3, events.HL removed (and LH 2 -> 1): a start-up transient. At the Wave-1 gate the shot has an L-H transition at 0.06 s and an H-L back-transition at 0.194 s while the density ramps up, and the real L-H transition at 25.6 s. The blip is gone from the Miller volume and surface commit 0e9e679 on (commits 76beb92, 4ead482 and 168b9b9 still have it; the ITER 0D volume and surface change from the ellipse formulas to the Miller integrals of the LCFS shape, and the crossing of P_L and the Martin threshold, which scales with S^0.941, at the low density of the ramp-up is marginal). The same commit moves the real L-H transition to 25.0 s, and the ITER density re-base (4d62b86) to 28.4 s (ELMs 186 -> 134, sawtooth crashes 50 -> 45). The mechanism of the blip itself was not analysed further: a 0.13 s H-mode episode during the density ramp-up that a marginal balance produced and a marginally different one does not. - DIIID15, 'GS updates rejected' 0 -> 1, warnings 1 -> 2, events.warning added: the low-density branch of the L-H threshold, (n_min/n)^2 instead of n_min/n (commit c202c5a; bisected: no rejection up to aad6293, one from c202c5a on, where the first L-H transition of the ramp-up moves from 0.06 to 0.101 s). The equilibrium update at t = 0.38 s stalls in every retry stage (residual 2.3e-2 after 120 iterations, 3 attempts): 'Grad-Shafranov update rejected - geometry held at the equilibrium of t = 0.25 s'; the shot still ends as scheduled (1 of 10 updates rejected). It is a robustness observation of the equilibrium solver at a state of the ramp-up, not a physics change; it is passed to ws3s and ws4i (a check of this update against the ws4 solver and its current-table iteration). - MASTU15, events.warning removed, events.sawtooth 33 added: the MAST-U first-campaign preset (commit 4d62b86: R 0.8 m, a 0.5 m, kappa 2.1, delta 0.47, 0.75 MA, 0.55 T, 2 MW NBI; q95 17.6 -> 6.4), not the L-H exponent (bisected: identical events at the Wave-1 gate and at c202c5a, changed at 4d62b86). The old preset had a hollow current at beta_p of 1 to 2 where the table-mode Grad-Shafranov solve stalled (3 of 7 updates rejected and a 'did not fully converge' warning, no sawtooth); with q95 6.4 those warnings are gone, sawtooth crashes occur (a q = 1 surface exists) and ELMs 200 -> 297; the remaining warning is the negative net electricity (Q_eng < 1).

## 2026-09-29 08:02 UTC — ws3s stage B: the 1.5D transport step is TR-BDF2 with error control (ProfileSettings.rtol 1e-2, atol 1e-4, dtMax 0.5 s) and Anderson-accelerated Picard iteration, and the ELM and the sawtooth crash end the step at the crossing of their threshold (Brent on the dense output of the step), instead of the backward-Euler step with its 8 % rule and events fired at the end of the step that crossed.

Causes, all of them intended and all in one commit series (ws3s stage B): (1) the time integration: TR-BDF2 (Bank et al., IEEE Trans. CAD 4 (1985) 436; Hosea and Shampine, Appl. Numer. Math. 20 (1996) 21), second order and L-stable, with the embedded error estimate filtered by the iteration matrix, a max-norm control on T_e, T_i, n_e and psi and an integral step-size controller; the mean step of ITER15 falls from 36 ms to 13 ms (steps 10959 -> 30260) and every golden 1.5D case takes 1.1 to 2.8 times as many steps (the 3 s start-up cases 1.1 to 1.2, DIIID15 2.3, MASTU15 2.0, DEMO15 2.5, ITER15 2.8). (2) The events: an ELM fires at the recovery time tau_E/8 (aimed 2 % beyond it) after the last one, not at the end of the step that passed it, and a sawtooth crash at the moment its trigger is first met (a q = 1 surface inside rho 0.8 with the shear above 0.2), so the ELM counts no longer follow the step (ITER15 1131 -> 1325, +17 %, at every tolerance and every dt limit: 1325 / 1326 / 1326 at rtol 1e-2 / 1e-3 / 1e-4, 1325 / 1325 / 1330 at dtMax 0.5 / 0.05 / 0.01 s); the backward-Euler ELMs were late by about half a step, which lengthened the period. (3) After an ELM or a sawtooth crash the step restarts at 0.5 ms (0.01 tau_E and 5 ms before) and the error control grows it. (4) The pressure of the work arrays is that of the new state (it was that of the last Picard iterate), so the alpha_ped of the diagnostics and of the ELM trigger are evaluated on the state the step ended in. (5) Picard iterations converge to 0.1 rtol with Anderson mixing (cgm transport: no longer oscillating). The 21 other cases are unchanged; every profile, every frame and every equilibrium digest of the nine 1.5D cases moves with the step, and the report gains the entry 'Transport steps (accepted / rejected by the error test)'.

The moves of the flat-top means (frame-weighted, the golden definition), old -> new, and the counts: ITER15 (400 s): Q 10.544 -> 10.548, P_fus 530.12 -> 530.09 MW, f_bs 0.2298 -> 0.2288, l_i 0.7381 -> 0.7413 (+0.4 %), T_ped 3.375 -> 3.410 keV (+1.0 %), q95 3.456 -> 3.453, q(0) 1.107 -> 1.138, alpha_ped/alpha_crit 0.819 -> 0.827, tau_E 2.326 -> 2.317 s, P_SOL 112.7 -> 111.4 MW, ELMs 1131 -> 1325, sawtooth crashes 25 -> 25, GS updates 25 accepted with 0 -> 2 retried. JET15 (5.5 s): Q 0.4448 -> 0.4447, T_ped 1.575 -> 1.566 keV (-0.5 %), f_bs 0.2482 -> 0.2490, q(0) 1.493 -> 1.739 (no sawtooth in this case: q(0) follows the current diffusion and the ELM sequence), ELMs 147 -> 153, GS updates 15 accepted, 3 -> 7 retried, 1 rejected; two new events, burn_start at 0.674 s and burn_end at 0.678 s (Q >= 1 for 4 ms at the end of the ramp, which the 3 ms steps of the old stepper stepped over). SPARC15 (10 s): Q 6.353 -> 6.379 (+0.4 %), T_ped 4.806 -> 4.816 keV, alpha_ped 0.977 -> 0.980, ELMs 14 -> 18. SPARC15-short (3 s): Q 5.161 -> 5.197 (+0.7 %), T_ped 5.133 -> 5.141 keV. DEMO15 (500 s): Q 23.363 -> 23.432 (+0.3 %), T_ped 3.773 -> 3.804 keV (+0.8 %), ELMs 1109 -> 1301, GS updates 31 accepted, rejected 2 -> 0 (one warning fewer). SPARC15-DHe3: Q 3.401e-3 -> 3.359e-3 (-1.2 %), T_ped 0.995 -> 0.993 keV. SPARC15-pB11: Q 5.370e-5 -> 5.254e-5 (-2.2 %), T_ped 2.316 -> 2.314 keV; the L-H transition at 0.1275 s is followed 0.2 ms later by an H-L back-transition (P_loss 38.2 MW -> 10.9 MW, below 0.7 P_LH: the loss power lags the heating step of the start-up) and the L-H returns at 1.399 s: L-H events 1 -> 2, H-L 0 -> 1. DIIID15 (3 s): Q 2.984e-4 -> 2.987e-4, T_ped 0.560 -> 0.566 keV (+1.1 %), q(0) 1.264 -> 1.280, alpha_ped 1.120 -> 1.144, ELMs 252 -> 267, GS updates rejected 1 -> 0 (its warning is no longer issued). MASTU15 (documented poor case, do not quote): Q 1.98e-4 -> 1.58e-4 (-20 %), f_bs 0.440 -> 0.328, l_i 0.781 -> 0.709, T_ped 0.115 -> 0.132 keV, q95 17.2 -> 25.3, q(0) 2.14 -> 0.90, ELMs 205 -> 239; the cause is the branch of the Grad-Shafranov retry ladder (accepted / retried / rejected 7 / 5 / 4 -> 2 / 2 / 6): the geometry stays near the start-up equilibrium and the q profile and the bootstrap current follow the few updates that are accepted, as in the entries of ws3d and ws3s stage A.

Convergence (bench:convergence, ITER15 400 s, flat-top means, nRho 50 unless stated): rtol 1e-2 / 1e-3 / 1e-4: Q 10.56 / 10.60 / 10.59, f_bs 0.2286 / 0.2294 / 0.2289, l_i 0.7431 / 0.7399 / 0.7422, T_ped 3.401 / 3.408 / 3.404 keV; dtMax 0.5 / 0.05 / 0.01 s (rtol 1e-2): Q 10.56 / 10.55 / 10.59 (it drifted 10.54 -> 10.63 with the backward-Euler step), T_ped 3.401 / 3.404 / 3.413; nRho 25 / 50 / 100: Q 10.32 / 10.56 / 10.62, T_ped 3.603 / 3.401 / 3.412 (50 -> 100: Q 0.6 %, f_bs 0.7 %, l_i 0.1 %, T_ped 0.3 %). The cgm transport of ITER15 runs 10 s of ramp-up in about 4 s of CPU time (30 s before), with 1.9 % of the attempts repeated (a third failed before).
