## 2026-09-29 08:27 UTC — merge of v4/ws7a (edge physics) onto v4/integration (ws9b, ws10a, ws10w, ws10d, ws7c, ws8, ws10b, ws2c already merged): 64 edge-channel keys are added to the nine magnetic 0D cases and to the nine 1.5D cases, and the 1.5D P_SOL becomes the ELM-inclusive balance; re-recorded on top of the ws2c golden files (the lane's own golden files, made on b059097, are not used); the moves the two ws7a entries above did not list (Divertor q_max, ELM-phase knock-ons, GS update rejections) are given

1. Keys added, no value moved (the nine magnetic 0D cases: ITER, JET, SPARC, DIIID, JT60SA, MASTU, DEMO, ITER-DHe3, ITER-pB11; the ledger below shows '0 keys moved' for each of them, so P_SOL, n_e, T and every other 0D number is bit for bit that of the ws2c re-record). The keys are the edge diagnostics of src/physics/edge: ten channels (P_sep_R, lambda_q, T_u, T_t, q_peak, f_pwr, detach, cz_det, q_det, p_div) with their history statistics and flat-top means, and the numeric entries of the report's engineering table (P_sep/R, target T_e, target q_peak, c_z for detachment). 64 keys per case, 63 in JET, SPARC, DIIID, JT60SA and ITER-pB11: there the c_z row of the report is the text 'n/a (> 100 %)' (or, for ITER-pB11, '>= X (> 100 % in N % of the flat top)'), and the harness records numeric scalars only; the text forms are pinned by src/physics/edge/integration.test.ts. Flat-top values of the new channels (P_sep/R MW/m, lambda_q mm, T_t eV, q_peak MW/m2, state 0 attached / 1 partially / 2 detached, c_z for detachment as a fraction): ITER 19.13, 0.567, 283, 26.4, attached, 0.398; JET 11.97, 1.276, 174, 7.3, attached, capped at 1; SPARC 29.5, 0.276, 319, 83.4, attached, capped; DIIID 8.87, 2.32, 49, 3.1, attached, capped; JT60SA 13.5, 1.12, 111, 9.6, attached, capped; MASTU 2.52, 4.6, 0.5, 0.02, detached, 0 (a detached prescribed-radiation run needs no seed); DEMO 41.5, 0.713, 676, 45.5, attached, 0.788; ITER-DHe3 1.34, 0.567, 1.6, 0.36, detached, 0.006; ITER-pB11 2.33, 0.854, 96, 2.0, partially detached, 0.050. Stellarator, ICF, MTF, Z-pinch, FRC, mirror and muon cases have no such channels and are unchanged (W7X and the 11 others: ok). The goldens here are recorded with the c_z fix of the lane's 06:48 entry (cz_det is 0 for a detached prescribed-radiation run, and the row is text where the requirement is capped).

2. Values moved (the nine 1.5D cases, on top of the 64 (63) added keys), one cause: acceptStep updates the lagged P_SOL after the smoothed dW/dt (ctx.dWdtS, which includes the energy the ELM crashes took out of the plasma) and passes that instead of the step-wise dW/dt of the accepted step. P_SOL is now the ELM-averaged power across the separatrix P_heat - P_rad - dW/dt, with the same dW/dt as the loss power of the confinement time. It feeds q_div, the legacy conduction-limited T_sep (proportional to P_SOL^(2/7)) and, for a tungsten source, the W source. Flat-top means, old (golden of the ws2c re-record) -> new: ITER15 P_SOL 113.85 -> 124.07 MW (+9.0 %; the balance P_heat - P_rad - dW/dt is 122.0 MW), q_div 26.86 -> 29.27 MW/m2, T_sep 0.3729 -> 0.3861 keV (+3.5 %), Q 10.408 -> 10.397 (-0.11 %), P_fus 523.2 -> 522.6 MW, T_ped 3.628 -> 3.670 keV (+1.2 %), tau_E +0.04 %, f_bs +0.04 %, q(0) 1.033 -> 1.038; JET15 P_SOL 30.20 -> 35.73 MW (+18.3 %), q_div 7.10 -> 8.40, T_sep 0.2277 -> 0.2400 keV (+5.4 %), Q 0.4413 -> 0.4402; DEMO15 P_SOL 422.3 -> 439.9 MW (+4.2 %), q_div 52.8 -> 55.0, Q 23.457 -> 23.407 (-0.22 %), flat-top T_sep unchanged at its 0.5 keV clamp (history mean 0.4929 -> 0.4947); DIIID15 P_SOL 13.88 -> 14.94 MW (+7.6 %), T_sep 0.1695 -> 0.1732 keV; MASTU15 P_SOL 1.854 -> 1.888 MW (+1.9 %); SPARC15 unchanged in the flat top (P_SOL 47.4804 -> 47.4785, no ELMs in its flat top); without ELMs the smoothed dW/dt lags a ramp-up by tau_E, so the short and the non-H-mode ramp-up cases move down: SPARC15-short P_SOL 38.23 -> 33.12 MW (-13.4 %, T_sep -3.9 %), SPARC15-pB11 10.48 -> 8.27 MW (-21.1 %, T_sep -6.6 %), SPARC15-DHe3 21.46 -> 20.95 MW (-2.4 %). Q and P_fus move by at most 0.3 % in every case (SPARC15-pB11 -0.30 %). The lane's own entry (01:59 above) reads 'SPARC15 unchanged'; that holds for the flat top only (see 3 and 4): SPARC15 has 2136 moved keys, transients and profile keys (history.P_SOL.max 49.44 -> 48.14 MW, history.V_loop.min -0.310 -> -0.063, history.Tsep.mean +1.2 %).

3. The report row 'Divertor q_max (MW/m2)' (the shot maximum of q_div, the legacy divertor load) moves in every 1.5D case and is not in the lane's entry. Old -> new: ITER15 43.8 -> 41.4 (-5.5 %), DEMO15 73.6 -> 56.2 (-23.6 %; history.P_SOL.max 588.6 -> 449.0 MW), JET15 11.5 -> 9.3 (-19.1 %; the report loses its warning 'Peak divertor heat flux 12 MW/m2 > 10 MW/m2' and the event at 0.71 s, the JET15 warning events go 2 -> 0 together with 4.), DIIID15 3.7 -> 3.5, SPARC15 85.8 -> 83.6, SPARC15-short 76.1 -> 68.7, SPARC15-DHe3 39.2 -> 36.7, SPARC15-pB11 22.0 -> 28.8 (+30.9 %). Cause: the step-wise dW/dt of the old P_SOL is spiky at an ELM crash (the stored energy drops in one step, P_SOL = P_heat - P_rad - dW/dt spikes), so the maximum over the shot was a crash spike; the smoothed dW/dt averages the crashes, the maximum falls to the level of the balance (DEMO15: 449 MW at t = 155.6 s in a quiet H-mode, against the 588.6 MW spike). SPARC15-pB11 is the opposite: its maximum is in the first 30 ms of the start-up, where W rises at 14.7 MW (step-wise dW/dt) but the smoothed dW/dt that lags by tau_E (0.69 s) reads 1.2 MW, so P_SOL is 16.6 MW instead of P_heat - P_rad - dW/dt = 3.2 MW (probed on the merged tree at t = 0.030 s: P_heat 19.56, P_rad 1.59, dWdt 14.73, dWdt_s 1.22). The same lag is the cause of the ramp-up moves in 2. It is the documented consequence of using the loss-power dW/dt for P_SOL, not a change to fix here; the peak load of a ramp-up window is a transient quantity.

4. Knock-ons of the boundary change that are not model changes (ELM, sawtooth and Grad-Shafranov update phases follow the boundary; the profile keys, 2100-2300 per 1.5D case, are the same phase-following as in the ws3d entries above): ELM count ITER15 1118 -> 1123, DEMO15 1120 -> 1118, DIIID15 251 -> 250, MASTU15 297 -> 296 (JET15 146 unchanged); sawtooth crashes ITER15 39 -> 37; the instantaneous end-of-shot pedestal temperature of the report extras (T_ped, final) ITER15 4.73 -> 3.46 keV, MASTU15 0.11 -> 0.12, DEMO15 5.24 -> 5.35 (it depends on where the ELM cycle is at the last frame; the flat-top mean T_ped moves +1.2 % and the history mean +0.3 %). Two Grad-Shafranov update rejections disappear: JET15 (2 of 16 updates rejected, at t = 0.93 s, residual 7.3e-3 after 120 iterations in the golden of the ws2c re-record) now has 15 accepted, 3 needing a retry, 0 rejected, and DIIID15 (1 of 10, at t = 0.38 s, residual 2.3e-2) 9 accepted, 1 retry, 0 rejected; the rejected instants were start-up transients whose pressure profile depends on the boundary, so this is the changed boundary state at the update instants, not a change of the equilibrium solver; the warning events (JET15 2 -> 0, DIIID15 1 -> 0) and the report warnings ('Grad-Shafranov: N of M equilibrium updates were rejected', JET15 4 -> 2 warnings, DIIID15 2 -> 1) follow. history.V_loop.min moves in several cases (SPARC15 -0.310 -> -0.063, DIIID15 -0.291 -> -1.323, DEMO15 -0.234 -> -0.298 V): it is the extremum of a per-step quantity (2 pi times the change of the boundary flux over the accepted step), which lands on another step when the ELM, sawtooth or equilibrium-update phase moves; it is not a flat-top quantity.

5. Not in this suite: the opt-in boundary ProfileSettings.edgeModel = 'twoPoint' (no golden case sets it; ITER15 with it: T_sep 0.384 -> 0.392 keV, Q 10.331 -> 10.328 in the lane's measurement) and the POPCON edge maps computePopcon(cfg, { edge: true }).

## 2026-09-29 08:47 UTC — merge of v4/ws5s (scenario engine, mode-flip frame diagnostics) onto v4/integration (ws9b, ws10a, ws10w, ws10d, ws7c, ws8, ws10b, ws2c, ws7a already merged): the ws5s re-record of 01:29 UTC redone on the merged base; four 0D cases (JET, SPARC, DIIID, MASTU) move in history frame statistics only, the other 26 are bit-identical

Cause. MagneticModel.postStep (0D) sets hmode (L-H, H-L) or ntm (sawtooth-seeded NTM onset, decay) after the step, but diagnostics() returned the pre-flip cache of the step's last rhs evaluation, so the frame recorded by that step showed the post-flip H_mode next to the pre-flip tau_E, P_cond and P_ELM. postStep now marks the cache stale at an ELM crash and at every flip and refreshes it once (refreshDiagnostics) before the burn logic and before a disruption or scheduled end can change the phase. The run itself is bitwise unchanged; only the diagnostics stored in a flip frame move.

Why four cases and not the five of the ws5s entry. A frame carries the flip only when the step that flips ends at an output boundary (a frame at the flip time exists). After the ws2c and ws7a merges the ITER L-H moved from 9.867 s to 9.383 s and falls inside an output interval, so no frame is recorded at the flip and ITER (like JT60SA at 0.5167 s and DEMO at 16.25 s, which never had one) is unchanged; the frames after a mid-interval flip are evaluated in the post-flip state in any case. The L-H flip frames that changed (merged base; tau_E [s], P_cond, P_ELM and P_transport [MW]; old -> new): JET (0.4363 s) tau_E 0.25385 -> 0.32626, P_cond 11.269 -> 6.1373, P_ELM 0 -> 2.6303, P_transport 11.269 -> 8.7676; SPARC (1.3667 s) tau_E 0.44193 -> 0.81897, P_cond 20.812 -> 7.8613, P_ELM 0 -> 3.3691, P_transport 20.812 -> 11.230; DIIID (0.18 s) tau_E 0.11397 -> 0.14899, P_cond 3.0879 -> 1.6534, P_ELM 0 -> 0.70862, P_transport 3.0879 -> 2.3621; MASTU (0.05 s) tau_E 0.044013 -> 0.069222, P_cond 0.60791 -> 0.27057, P_ELM 0 -> 0.11596, P_transport 0.60791 -> 0.38652. The pre-flip values were the L-mode ones (P_ELM is off in L-mode), which is what a 0D flip frame used to show next to H_mode = 1.

Headline moves, all frame statistics over the whole history (old -> new, relative). JET: tauE.min 0.25385 -> 0.25528 (+5.6e-3; the old flip frame was the minimum), tauE.mean 0.281881 -> 0.281930 (+1.7e-4), P_ELM.mean 9.52490 -> 9.52665 (+1.8e-4), P_cond.mean 22.6115 -> 22.6081 (-1.5e-4), P_transport.mean -5.2e-5, lawson.mean and triple.mean +5.5e-5. SPARC: tauE.mean 0.554354 -> 0.554605 (+4.5e-4), P_cond.mean -2.6e-4, P_ELM.mean +1.7e-4, lawson.mean and triple.mean +1.8e-4, P_transport.mean -1.4e-4. DIIID: tauE.mean 0.0868399 -> 0.0868632 (+2.7e-4), P_ELM.mean +9.3e-5, P_cond.mean -8.0e-5, lawson.mean and triple.mean +5.3e-5, P_transport.mean -2.9e-5. MASTU (largest moves): lawson.min 8.2400e-5 -> 8.4639e-5 and triple.min 2.4720e17 -> 2.5392e17 (+2.6e-2, the old flip frame was the minimum), tauE.mean 0.0430986 -> 0.0431238 (+5.8e-4), P_cond.min and P_transport.min -1.2e-3, P_cond.mean -1.9e-4, P_ELM.mean +1.5e-4, lawson.mean and triple.mean +1.2e-4, P_transport.mean -8.6e-5.

Verification. On the merged base with only the refresh of MagneticModel.postStep taken out (magnetic.ts of the previous integration head cf42433, everything else of the merge in place: scenario engine, kernel integration, the ws2c and ws7a numbers), golden matches all 30 cases of the previous re-record at 1e-9. Every move above therefore comes from that one refresh, and the scenario engine (a run without a scenario is bitwise unchanged) moves nothing. Nothing else moved: flatTop.*, the report scalars (Q, P_fus, E_fus, T_max, ...), the events, the ELM and sawtooth counts and every other history key are bit-identical; no 1.5D case, no pulsed or alternative-concept case changes (1.5D flip frames need no refresh: their frames carry the diagnostics the step wrote together with its own H_mode, documented in profiles/events/lh.ts and pinned in kernel/modeFlipFrames.test.ts).

## 2026-09-29 10:39 UTC — merge of v4/ws7b (systems-lite engineering) onto v4/integration (ws9b, ws10a, ws10w, ws10d, ws7c, ws8, ws10b, ws2c, ws7a, ws5s already merged): the engineering block of the 19 magnetic cases is re-derived on the merged base; only scalars.engineering.*, scalars.Q_eng and scalars.warnings.length move, everything the plasma model computes is bit-identical

Cause. The two ws7b entries above (01:46 and 06:59 UTC) describe the lane on its own base b059097; its golden files are not used here (the merge kept the golden files of the previous head and both entries whole), the numbers below are the merged base recomputed. The engineering block of the magnetic shot report is now written by src/physics/systems/assess.ts from the flat-top means of the run: (a) TF coil stress is the Tresca stress of the PROCESS-style three-layer plane-stress analysis of the inboard leg plus the vertical tension (Kovari et al., Fusion Eng. Des. 104 (2016) 9) instead of the thin-ring estimate; (b) the coil magnetic energy is the toroidal-cavity integral with the D-shaped coil height; (c) the cryoplant power of a superconducting coil is the Slack heat load (static + pulsed-field over the design pulse of the plant, 1055 s unless systems.pulseLength_s says otherwise + leads + TF nuclear heating) times the technology's cryo_W_per_W, replacing the flat 5 MW of the recirculating power; (d) TBR follows the Shimwell et al. (2016) fit as a function of the 6Li enrichment and the blanket depth of the new radial build instead of the flat 1.199; (e) new keys: TF coils, TF winding pack J, TF case and winding-pack Tresca, TF stress margin, TF vertical tension per coil, TF mass, CS flux swing, Flux required, Flux margin (tokamaks), TF nuclear heating, Cryo heat load, Cryoplant power and Cryo pulse length (superconducting coils), Inboard blanket and Inboard shield+vessel (with a blanket). Q_eng, Net P_electric, P_recirculating, LCOE and EROI move through (c) and through (b) for the copper coils (their recirculating term 0.02 x stored energy reads the new magnetic energy).

Which keys move. 19 cases: ITER, JET, SPARC, DIIID, JT60SA, MASTU, W7X, DEMO, ITER15, JET15, SPARC15, SPARC15-short, DEMO15, ITER-DHe3, ITER-pB11, SPARC15-DHe3, SPARC15-pB11, DIIID15, MASTU15 (the eleven non-magnetic cases are bit-identical). In each of them only scalars.engineering.* (333 keys moved or added), scalars.Q_eng (19) and scalars.warnings.length (7) differ: no history, flat-top, trace, event, geometry or profile key moves, and no other scalar (Q_sci, P_fus, E_fus, T, score, the events and the ELM and sawtooth counts are bit for bit the previous head). The set of moved keys per case is the set the lane's own re-record moved (checked case by case against the golden files of b059097 and bf4b163), except MASTU and MASTU15 where P_recirculating, EROI and Net P_electric round to the same number on the merged base.

Headline moves (old -> new). TF stress (MPa, limit of the technology in brackets): ITER 82 -> 489 (660), JET 27 -> 143, SPARC 197 -> 1024 (800: over the limit, +1 warning in the five SPARC cases), DIIID 13 -> 73, JT60SA 14 -> 97, MASTU 1 -> 6, W7X 29 -> 120, DEMO 133 -> 745 (660: over the limit, +1 warning in DEMO and DEMO15); SPARC and DEMO carry a permanent over-stress warning because their preset inboard legs are thin (SPARC 0.5 m, DEMO 1.0 m; the same model gives 571 MPa for the 1.4 m leg of the PROCESS DEMO build), a preset review, not a model question. Magnetic energy (GJ): ITER 37.69 -> 41.77, DEMO 141.41 -> 160.04, JET 1.69 -> 1.81, SPARC 3.86 -> 4.11, W7X 0.46 -> 0.73, JT60SA 1.04 -> 0.97, DIIID 0.18 -> 0.17; on the merged base the shot report passes the boundary (LCFS) shape of ws2c to the systems models (ITER kappa 1.85, coil leg height 10.0 m; the lane's own 39.6 GJ used the nominal 95 % shape, kappa 1.7, 9.4 m), and the TF Tresca stress does not depend on the elongation. TBR: ITER, ITER15, ITER-DHe3 and ITER-pB11 1.199 -> 1.125, DEMO and DEMO15 1.199 -> 1.143. Cryoplant power (new key, MW): ITER 32.6 (heat load 108.7 kW of which TF nuclear heating 44.5 kW), ITER15 32.9, DEMO 30.6, DEMO15 32.0, SPARC 28.2 (heat load 705.6 kW, nuclear heating 476 kW: the PROCESS fit for a DEMO breeding blanket applied to a build without blanket, the largest single doubt of the lane), SPARC15 23.4, SPARC15-short 19.3, JT60SA 3.8, W7X 8.7, ITER-DHe3 and ITER-pB11 13.2, SPARC15-DHe3 and SPARC15-pB11 0.6. Net P_electric (MW): ITER 40 -> 13, ITER15 45 -> 17, DEMO 452 -> 427, DEMO15 642 -> 615, SPARC -22 -> -45, SPARC15 -31 -> -50, SPARC15-short -40 -> -54, JET -455 -> -480 and JET15 -453 -> -478 (copper coil term), ITER-DHe3 -194 -> -202, ITER-pB11 -184 -> -192, SPARC15-DHe3 -86 -> -81, SPARC15-pB11 -84 -> -80, W7X -41 -> -45, DIIID -89 -> -86, JT60SA -115 -> -113. Q_eng: ITER 1.224 -> 1.061, ITER15 1.255 -> 1.085, DEMO 2.227 -> 2.083, DEMO15 2.638 -> 2.468, SPARC 0.781 -> 0.634, SPARC15 0.678 -> 0.570, SPARC15-short 0.582 -> 0.506, JET 0.0326 -> 0.0310, W7X 0.0600 -> 0.0553, ITER-DHe3 0.1246 -> 0.1201, ITER-pB11 0.1233 -> 0.1187, SPARC15-DHe3 0.1037 -> 0.1087, SPARC15-pB11 0.1026 -> 0.1076, DIIID 0.0572 -> 0.0588. LCOE ($/MWh): ITER 2654 -> 8448, ITER15 2356 -> 6139 (net power is only 13 to 17 MW, so the cost per MWh is very sensitive to the cryoplant), DEMO 787 -> 835, DEMO15 555 -> 579. EROI: ITER 1.2 -> 1.1, SPARC 0.8 -> 0.6, DEMO 2.2 -> 2.1.

Why the numbers differ from the lane's own entry. The lane's ITER on b059097 gave Q_eng 1.076, net 16 MW, cryoplant 32.1 MW and LCOE 6726 because its flat-top mean fusion power was 523.4 MW; the merged base has 513.7 MW (ws2c time-weighted flat top, ws7a and ws5s knock-ons), so the recirculating power (5 % of P_fus house load, TF nuclear heating proportional to P_fus) and the net power follow; the machine part of the cryo heat load (static + pulsed-field + leads) is 64.2 kW against 63.1 kW, the difference being the larger coil of the boundary shape. Flux margin is negative for every tokamak preset (ITER -0.231, JET -0.53, SPARC -0.77, DEMO +0.62): the preset radial build does not resolve the solenoid, so the CS keys are an estimate and the check warns only when a design gives systems.cs (open item for the preset owner).

## 2026-09-29 10:57 UTC — ws3s stage C: the current diffusion is in the Hinton-Hazeltine form F^2 d/drho(V' g2 psi'/F) (the F' term of the poloidal current had the wrong sign), the plasma current is a boundary-condition input (control Ip_MA, programme), and a predictive transport model is solved by Newton; the nine 1.5D cases move

Only the form of the current-diffusion equation moves numbers of the golden cases. The rest of the stage is bit for bit: with the old form restored in the working tree, ITER15 and DEMO15 reproduce the previous golden files exactly (the control Ip_MA at its default, the boundary current as an input of each stage, the Newton solver that the 'scaling' cases do not use, and the moving-coordinate term that the model passes as 0 change nothing). The v3 form (1/(mu0 V')) d/drho(V' F g2 psi') and the Hinton-Hazeltine form F^2/(mu0 V') d/drho(V' g2 psi'/F) agree for a constant F and differ by 2 F' X/(mu0 V') in <j.B> (X = V' g2 psi'); on the analytic Solov'ev equilibrium of geometry.test.ts the new form reproduces <j.B> = F p' + F FF' <R^-2>/mu0 + FF' g2 psi'^2/(F mu0) to 5e-5 and the old one is off by 1.2 %. The skin-time response of a uniform cylinder to a step of I_p is checked against the Bessel series to 2e-3 of I_p (currentDiffusion.test.ts).

Flat-top means, frame-weighted (the golden definition), previous stage -> this one. ITER15 (400 s, steps 30260 -> 30379): Q 10.548 -> 10.649 (+1.0 %), P_fus 530.1 -> 535.7 MW, f_bs 0.2288 -> 0.2300, l_i 0.7413 -> 0.7391, T_ped 3.410 -> 3.421 keV (+0.3 %), q95 3.453 -> 3.464, q(0) 1.138 -> 1.268, alpha_ped/alpha_crit 0.827 -> 0.826, sawtooth crashes 25 -> 21, ELMs 1325 -> 1326, GS updates 25 accepted, retried 2 -> 4. JET15: Q 0.4447 -> 0.4449, f_bs 0.2490 -> 0.2481, T_ped 1.566 -> 1.567 keV, q(0) 1.739 -> 1.728, steps 3235 -> 3184, GS retried 7 -> 4. SPARC15 (10 s): Q 6.379 -> 6.326 (-0.8 %), T_ped 4.816 -> 4.809 keV, ELMs 18 -> 10, steps 1310 -> 1218. SPARC15-short: Q 5.197 -> 5.180 (-0.3 %), T_ped 5.141 -> 5.146 keV. DEMO15 (500 s): Q 23.43 -> 23.45, f_bs 0.4287 -> 0.4281, T_ped 3.804 -> 3.811 keV, q(0) 1.439 -> 1.745 (the move is in the first cells of the q profile of the last frame: 1.34 -> 1.66 at the axis, 1.29 -> 1.44 in the second cell, 1.23 -> 1.27 in the third, and under 1 % from the fifth cell on; there is no sawtooth in this case, and the axis of a 500 s discharge is still relaxing with a skin time of a few hundred seconds), GS updates 31 -> 30 accepted, 12 -> 13 retried, 0 -> 1 rejected (one more warning). SPARC15-DHe3: Q 3.36e-3 -> 3.37e-3, T_ped 0.993 -> 0.999 keV. SPARC15-pB11: Q 5.25e-5 -> 5.38e-5 (+2.4 %), T_ped 2.314 -> 2.335 keV; the L-H / H-L blip of the start-up stays. DIIID15: Q 2.987e-4 -> 2.99e-4, T_ped 0.566 keV, q(0) 1.280 -> 1.310, GS retried 4 -> 5.

MASTU15 (the documented poor case, do not quote) now ends in a beta-limit disruption at 0.68 s instead of the scheduled end at 2 s: the equilibrium of the shot flips between two Grad-Shafranov solutions (q95 24 or 17) as the updates are accepted, the stored energy grows to 0.35 MJ with the better confinement of the low q95 one, and beta_N crosses the Troyon limit of 5.5 at 0.631 s (4.7 at 0.6 s), a knife edge that any change of the numbers moves (Q 1.58e-4 -> 1.50e-4, f_bs 0.328 -> 0.565, q95 25.3 -> 19.1, q(0) 0.90 -> 3.25, steps 3342 -> 1317, ELMs 239 -> 71, GS accepted / retried / rejected 2 / 2 / 6 -> 4 / 4 / 1; a run at 0.65 of the heating power ends as scheduled and keeps the gate test of integrity.test.ts). The 21 other cases are unchanged.

Convergence (bench:convergence, ITER15 400 s, flat-top means, nRho 50 unless stated): nRho 25 / 50 / 100: Q 10.41 / 10.65 / 10.65, f_bs 0.2333 / 0.2300 / 0.2307, l_i 0.7142 / 0.7391 / 0.7388, T_ped 3.590 / 3.421 / 3.427 keV (50 -> 100: 0.02, 0.29, 0.04, 0.19 %); rtol 1e-2 / 1e-3 / 1e-4: Q 10.65 / 10.68 / 10.68, f_bs 0.2300 / 0.2309 / 0.2312, l_i 0.7391 / 0.7353 / 0.7353, T_ped 3.421 / 3.439 / 3.426 keV (at most 0.34, 0.51, 0.51, 0.53 % from 1e-2); dtMax 0.5 / 0.05 / 0.01 s: Q 10.65 / 10.66 / 10.69, T_ped 3.421 / 3.433 / 3.437 keV, ELMs 1326 / 1326 / 1331. The cgm transport of ITER15 (Newton, the default of a predictive model) runs 10 s of ramp-up in 3.1 s of wall time with 1.7 % of the attempts rejected by the error test (2.0 % with the failed ones).

## 2026-09-29 11:17 UTC — merge of v4/ws4i (equilibrium: edge-clustered surface table, self-consistent Grad-Shafranov updates) onto v4/integration (ws9b, ws10a, ws10w, ws10d, ws7c, ws8, ws10b, ws2c, ws7a, ws5s, ws7b already merged): the nine 1.5D cases are re-recorded on the merged base; the 21 other cases are bit-identical

Cause. The ws4i entry above (06:35 UTC) describes the lane on its own base b059097; its golden files are not used here (the merge took the golden files of the previous head and kept both entries whole), the numbers below are the merged base recomputed against the previous head 51cede2. Two changes of the lane move the 1.5D numbers, both in the coupling of the transport to the fixed-boundary Grad-Shafranov solver: (1) ee239b3, the default flux-surface table of the solver has 101 nodes clustered at the edge (psi_N = s + s^2 - s^3, s = (k/100)^2) instead of 51 at (k/50)^2, so the outer-face metrics g1, g2, V', q and rho_tor that the transport geometry is built from are within 0.5 % of a 401-surface table (they were up to 14.7 % off) and the table's q95 is correct (+0.6 % ITER, +1.1 % SPARC, +8.8 % MASTU at a fixed shape: l_i and beta_p do not depend on the table); (2) 7673e8d, the update of the equilibrium is an outer iteration of the pressure and current tables on the new equilibrium's own flux surfaces (profiles/coupling/outer.ts) instead of a single solve on tables mapped through the previous equilibrium with an accept/reject retry ladder. On the merged base two integrator commits sit on top of the lane and are part of this re-record: 230080c adopts the best equilibrium of an outer iteration that did not reach its tolerance up to a mapping mismatch of 1e-2 (half a transport cell) instead of 5e-3 (JET15 lost the update at t = 0.557 s of its ramp-up on the merged base, mismatch 5.2e-3 against 5e-3, because ws2c and ws7a shifted its trajectory), and builds the new geometry on the radial grid of the geometry it replaces (bit for bit the uniform grid on this base, so it moves no number here; it matters once the packed grid of ws3s is merged).

Which keys move. The nine 1.5D cases: ITER15, JET15, SPARC15, SPARC15-short, DEMO15, SPARC15-DHe3, SPARC15-pB11, DIIID15, MASTU15 (2139 to 2317 keys each: the equilibrium, the profiles, and through the geometry the history, flat-top and scalar statistics). The 21 other cases (all 0D, and the stellarator, pulsed and mirror cases) are bit-identical to the previous head, as the lane measured on its own base.

Headline moves against the previous head (whole-run scalars; the last Grad-Shafranov equilibrium): - ITER15: Q_sci_avg 10.93 -> 10.83 (-0.96 %), E_fusion -0.96 %, Ti max 12.157 -> 12.081 keV, Loop voltage 0.059 -> 0.057 V, Shafranov shift 0.164 -> 0.177 m, ELM count 1123 -> 1108, q95(GS) 3.418 -> 3.537 (+3.5 %), l_i(GS) 0.7513 -> 0.7048 (-6.2 %), beta_p(GS) +1.5 %. - JET15: Q_sci_avg 0.4659 -> 0.4653 (-0.13 %), Ti max 4.691 -> 4.697 keV, GS updates 15 accepted, 3 -> 1 needing a retry, 0 rejected (with the 1e-2 limit; against 5e-3 the update at t = 0.557 s would be rejected), q95(GS) 3.671 -> 3.760 (+2.4 %), l_i(GS) 0.7695 -> 0.7394 (-3.9 %). - SPARC15: Q_sci_avg 5.480 -> 5.461 (-0.34 %), q95(GS) 4.087 -> 4.205 (+2.9 %), l_i(GS) 0.7663 -> 0.7343 (-4.2 %). SPARC15-short: Q_sci_avg 2.939 -> 2.947 (+0.28 %), Loop voltage 0.033 -> 0.028 V (0.036 -> 0.031 V on the lane's own base as well), q95(GS) +3.2 %, l_i(GS) -4.3 %. - DEMO15: Q_sci_avg 21.69 -> 21.75 (+0.29 %), Loop voltage 0.014 -> 0.011 V, Shafranov shift +4.3 %, q95(GS) 4.855 -> 5.039 (+3.8 %), l_i(GS) 0.7708 -> 0.7186 (-6.8 %). - SPARC15-DHe3 Q_sci_avg -0.30 %, SPARC15-pB11 -0.44 %, DIIID15 -0.36 % (GS updates needing a retry 1 -> 0: the update that ws2c's L-H exponent had made hard, recorded as open in the ws2c merge, is solved without a retry by the outer iteration; the worst adopted mapping mismatch of the shot is 1.5e-3), q95(GS) +1.8 %, l_i(GS) -2.7 %; MASTU15 (the first-campaign preset of ws2c) Q_sci_avg -0.37 %, q95(GS) 6.264 -> 6.381 (+1.9 %), 6 GS updates accepted before and after. - GS updates accepted / needing a retry / rejected, before -> after: ITER15 25/0/0 -> 25/0/0, JET15 15/3/0 -> 15/1/0, SPARC15 30/0/0 unchanged, SPARC15-short 17/0/0 unchanged, DEMO15 31/0/0 unchanged, SPARC15-DHe3 13/0/0 unchanged, SPARC15-pB11 14/0/0 unchanged, DIIID15 9/1/0 -> 9/0/0, MASTU15 6/0/0 unchanged. No update is rejected in any golden case.

The equilibrium of the last update is now consistent with the transport's own profiles: q95(GS) / q95(transport, flat-top mean) is 1.008 (ITER15), 1.015 (JET15), 1.000 (SPARC15), 1.013 (SPARC15-short), 1.010 (DEMO15), 0.999 (SPARC15-DHe3), 0.995 (SPARC15-pB11), 0.998 (DIIID15) and 0.998 (MASTU15), within 1.5 % everywhere (the lane measured up to 15 % before); l_i(GS) against the transport l_i is within 3 % (up to 8 % before). The 4 to 7 % drop of l_i(GS) and the 2 to 4 % rise of q95(GS) of the large machines are therefore the correction of a stale mapping, not a change of the transport model; the transport-side scalars move by at most 1 % (ITER15) and mostly by 0.1 to 0.4 %, through the geometry (V', g1, g2, q of the outer cells) that the transport reads from the equilibrium.

## 2026-09-29 12:19 UTC — ws3s review fix: the initial poloidal flux of the 1.5D model carries exactly I_p (the equilibrium's own current is 1.0006 to 1.0011 I_p, which left a negative current in the outermost cell of the edge-packed grid at t = 0); the nine 1.5D cases move

Only the normalisation of the initial psi moves numbers of the golden cases. ProfileModel.initialState builds psi from the q profile of the Grad-Shafranov tables, and the current carried by those tables is I_p only to the accuracy of the solver's line integrals: eq.prof.Ienc at the last surface is 1.00106 I_p on ITER15, 1.00108 on JET15, 1.00105 on SPARC15, 1.00124 on DIIID15. The current equation takes I_p as its boundary condition, so the enclosed current at the last interior face came out above I_p (t = 0, edge-packed grid: ITER15 1.00058 I_p, JET15 1.00054, SPARC15 1.00063, DEMO15 1.00069, DIIID15 1.00108) and the outermost cell carried a negative current (its packed width holds 5e-4 I_p; the uniform grid's cell, 2 % of the radius, swallowed the mismatch). The initial psi' is now scaled by I_p over the enclosed current of the equilibrium's last surface (qprofile.ts equilibriumCurrentScale): the enclosed current at the last interior face is 0.99953 (ITER15), 0.99946 (JET15), 0.99958 (SPARC15), 0.99953 (DEMO15), 0.99984 (DIIID15), every cell of the t = 0 current profile is positive (initialCurrent.test.ts), and q of the initial state is 0.1 % higher. The same commit range holds the check of rtol, atol and dtMax (profiles/settings.ts), which changes nothing for a valid setting. Verified: with the scale forced to 1 in the working tree ITER15, JET15, DEMO15 and DIIID15 reproduce the previous golden files exactly, so the whole move below is the normalisation of the initial psi.

The moves are the response of the slowly relaxing core current and of the event timing to a 0.1 % change of the initial current profile; the diagnostics that carry them are q(0), the sawtooth count and the step count; Q, f_bs, l_i and T_ped move by less than 0.5 % in every case except MASTU15. Flat-top means, frame-weighted (the golden definition), previous record -> this one. ITER15 (400 s, steps 30379 -> 30270): Q 10.649 -> 10.643 (-0.05 %), P_fus 535.7 -> 535.4 MW, f_bs 0.2300 -> 0.2304, l_i 0.7391 -> 0.7361, T_ped 3.421 -> 3.430 keV (+0.3 %), q95 3.464 -> 3.468, q(0) 1.268 -> 1.089, alpha_ped/alpha_crit 0.826 -> 0.827, sawtooth crashes 21 -> 26 (period 19.1 -> 15.4 s), ELMs 1326 -> 1326, GS retried 4 -> 2. JET15 (5.5 s, steps 3184 -> 3228): Q 0.4449 -> 0.4444, f_bs 0.2481 -> 0.2480, l_i 0.7651 -> 0.7645, T_ped 1.567 -> 1.566 keV, q(0) 1.728 -> 1.525, ELMs 153 -> 153, S_fuel 0.366 -> 0.451, GS retried 4 -> 2. SPARC15 (10 s, steps 1218 -> 1202): Q 6.326 -> 6.321, T_ped 4.809 -> 4.809 keV, q(0) 0.854 -> 0.855, ELMs 10 -> 9. SPARC15-short: Q 5.180 -> 5.180, T_ped 5.146 -> 5.148 keV, q(0) 1.011 -> 1.012. DEMO15 (500 s, steps 35954 -> 35890): Q 23.446 -> 23.442, f_bs 0.4281 -> 0.4288, l_i 0.7457 -> 0.7446, T_ped 3.811 -> 3.809 keV, q95 4.910 -> 4.906, q(0) 1.745 -> 1.500 (there is no sawtooth in this case; the q profile of the last frame moves at the axis, 1.66 -> 1.36 in the first cell, and by 3.6 % at the edge; the mid frame at 250 s moves by 0.8 % at the axis: the axis region of a 500 s discharge is still relaxing and its q(0) is the least converged number of the record, as it was at the previous re-record, 1.439 -> 1.745), V_loop 0.01053 -> 0.01128 V, GS retried / rejected 13 / 1 -> 11 / 2. SPARC15-DHe3: Q 3.374e-3 -> 3.373e-3. SPARC15-pB11: Q 5.381e-5 -> 5.359e-5 (-0.4 %), T_ped 2.335 -> 2.336 keV. DIIID15 (3 s, steps 7558 -> 7444): Q 2.988e-4 -> 2.990e-4, f_bs 0.3109 -> 0.3107, l_i 0.8139 -> 0.8153, T_ped 0.566 -> 0.568 keV, q(0) 1.310 -> 1.254, ELMs 267 -> 267, GS retried 5 -> 3.

MASTU15 (the documented poor case, do not quote) is back on the other branch of its knife edge: it ends as scheduled at 2 s (steps 1317 -> 3581, ELMs 71 -> 240, Q 1.50e-4 -> 1.86e-4, f_bs 0.565 -> 0.381, q95 19.1 -> 18.9, q(0) 3.25 -> 1.57, GS accepted / retried / rejected 4 / 4 / 1 -> 6 / 5 / 6) instead of the beta-limit disruption at 0.68 s that the previous record had (the shot flips between two Grad-Shafranov solutions, q95 24 or 17, and beta_N sits at 4.7 against the Troyon limit of 5.5; any change of the numbers moves it, as recorded at the previous re-record). The initial current of this preset still has a negative outermost cell (the last interval of its surface table, 0.886 to 1 in rho, is coarse for a q that diverges at the X-point: eq.prof.Ienc at the last surface is 1.0022 I_p, and after the scaling the enclosed current at the last interior face is still 1.003 I_p on the packed grid, 1.008 on the uniform one); it did in the base on the uniform grid, and the fix belongs with the resolution of the surface table (lane ws4i). The 21 other cases are unchanged.

## 2026-09-29 13:04 UTC — merge of v4/ws3s (1.5D solver: edge-packed radial grid, TR-BDF2 with error control, event localisation, Newton and Pereverzev-Corrigan stage solvers, Hinton-Hazeltine current diffusion, initial-current normalisation and edge matching) onto v4/integration (ws9b, ws10a, ws10w, ws10d, ws7c, ws8, ws10b, ws2c, ws7a, ws5s, ws7b, ws4i, ws10e already merged): the nine 1.5D cases are re-recorded on the merged base; the 21 other cases are bit-identical

Cause. The four ws3s entries above (01:44, 08:02, 10:57 and 12:19 UTC) describe the lane on its own base b059097; its golden files are not used here (the merge took the golden files of the previous head and kept every entry whole), the numbers below are the merged base recomputed against the previous head dd2f5aa. The lane's changes, each with its share on this base: (1) the radial grid is edge-packed by default (ProfileSettings.gridPacking 4: tanh step in the cell density, edge cells 5 times narrower, 10 cells across the pedestal at nRho 50 instead of 3), T_ped is the temperature interpolated at rho_ped and the ELM alpha check includes the separatrix face: this is the whole move of T_ped (-8.5 % ITER15, -5.3 % JET15, -0.7 % SPARC15, -10.9 % DIIID15, -9.5 % DEMO15 against a run of the merged code with gridPacking 0) and about +2.2 % of ITER15 Q (10.470 -> 10.697), +0.9 % of JET15 Q and +0.6 % of DEMO15 Q; (2) the transport step is TR-BDF2 with an embedded error estimate and localised ELM and sawtooth events (the ELM count of ITER15 is 1318 with the uniform grid and 1332 with the packed one, 1325 to 1331 in the lane's own convergence runs, instead of following the step: 1108 on the previous head), which takes 1.1 to 3.4 times the steps (ITER15 10363 -> 21562, DEMO15 13211 -> 32708, DIIID15 3076 -> 7305, MASTU15 5624 -> 19061, JET15 1798 -> 3004); (3) the current diffusion is in the Hinton-Hazeltine form with the moving-coordinate term (the F' term of the v3 form had the wrong sign; the analytic Solov'ev <j.B> is reproduced to 5e-5, was 1.2 % off), which moves l_i, q(0) and the loop voltage (JET15 V_loop 0.074 -> 0.087 V with the uniform grid already 0.084, MASTU15 0.283 -> 0.344 V); (4) the initial poloidal flux carries exactly I_p (the equilibrium table's own enclosed current at the last surface is 1.0006 to 1.0013 I_p) and, in this merge, its edge follows the table's enclosed current from rho 0.9 to 0.97 (matchEdgeCurrent; with the 101-surface table of ws4i the q-based profile missed it by up to 1e-3 I_p and left the outermost cell 2.4 to 3.7 times its neighbour's): q(0) and the sawtooth count, which follow the slowly relaxing core current, move most under (3) and (4) together (DEMO15 q(0) -8.8 %, JET15 -6.4 %, DIIID15 -4.5 %, ITER15 crashes 38 -> 40; the shares of the two were not separated on this base); (5) Newton with Pereverzev-Corrigan fallback and the plasma-current control Ip_MA change no golden number (the presets are 'scaling' models, whose Picard iteration is bit-identical to before, and I_p is constant). Interaction with ws4i: the self-consistent Grad-Shafranov update removes the extra retries that the lane saw on the packed grid (DEMO15 11 to 13 of 31): all nine cases accept every update (25, 15, 30, 17, 31, 13, 14, 9 and 6 for ITER15, JET15, SPARC15, SPARC15-short, DEMO15, SPARC15-DHe3, SPARC15-pB11, DIIID15, MASTU15) with none retried and none rejected. Interaction with ws2c/ws4i on MASTU15: the preset is the first-campaign scenario, the lane's beta-limit disruption at 0.68 s does not occur and the shot ends as scheduled at 2 s (it does on the previous head as well). Interaction with ws7a: P_SOL of the merged base is that of the previous head (flat-top ITER15 127 MW against 124 MW; the lane alone, on the base before the edge work, had 112 MW): the request of the lane to feed the smoothed dW/dt to P_SOL is not applied here.

Which keys move. The nine 1.5D cases: ITER15, JET15, SPARC15, SPARC15-short, DEMO15, SPARC15-DHe3, SPARC15-pB11, DIIID15, MASTU15 (the equilibrium, the profiles, and through them the history, flat-top and scalar statistics). The 21 other cases (all 0D, and the stellarator, pulsed and mirror cases) are bit-identical to the previous head: none of the lane's changes touches them.

Headline moves against the previous head (time-weighted flat-top means unless noted): - ITER15 (steps 10363 -> 21562): Q 10.401 -> 10.697 (+2.8 %; whole-run Q_sci_avg 10.826 -> 10.959, +1.2 %), P_fus 522.7 -> 537.9 MW, T_ped 3.788 -> 3.491 keV (-7.8 %), l_i 0.7183 -> 0.7291 (+1.5 %), q(0) 1.053 -> 1.029, f_bs 0.2306 -> 0.2313, V_loop 0.0573 -> 0.0582 V, alpha_ped/alpha_crit 0.745 -> 0.792, P_SOL 124.0 -> 127.0 MW, ELM count 1108 -> 1332, sawtooth crashes 38 -> 40, GS updates 25 accepted / 0 / 0. - JET15 (1798 -> 3004): Q 0.4389 -> 0.4428 (+0.9 %), T_ped 1.696 -> 1.629 keV (-4.0 %), l_i 0.7609 -> 0.7635, q(0) 1.386 -> 1.298 (-6.4 %), V_loop 0.0740 -> 0.0871 V (+17.7 %), ELM 146 -> 153, GS retried 1 -> 0. - SPARC15 (836 -> 1090): Q 6.318 -> 6.290 (-0.4 %), T_ped 4.937 -> 4.906 keV, q(0) 0.874 -> 0.860, alpha_ped 0.798 -> 0.922, no ELM before or after. - SPARC15-short (820 -> 918): Q 5.230 -> 5.233, T_ped 5.277 -> 5.237, V_loop 0.0283 -> 0.0315 V. - DEMO15 (13211 -> 32708): Q 23.485 -> 23.499 (+0.06 %), T_ped 4.318 -> 3.875 keV (-10.3 %), l_i 0.7391 -> 0.7409, q(0) 1.613 -> 1.471 (-8.8 %), f_bs 0.4295 -> 0.4275, V_loop 0.01093 -> 0.00984 V, ELM 1110 -> 1318. - SPARC15-DHe3 (814 -> 938): Q 3.426e-3 -> 3.359e-3 (-2.0 %), T_ped 1.123 -> 1.004 keV (-10.6 %). - SPARC15-pB11 (821 -> 971): Q 4.555e-5 -> 4.625e-5 (+1.5 %), T_ped 2.486 -> 2.394 keV, alpha_ped 0.176 -> 0.311, one HL and two LH events instead of one LH (the loss power of this start-up lags the heating step: the L-H / H-L blip the lane reports on its own base). - DIIID15 (3076 -> 7305): Q 2.937e-4 -> 2.972e-4 (+1.2 %), T_ped 0.647 -> 0.593 keV (-8.3 %), l_i 0.8033 -> 0.8136, q(0) 1.256 -> 1.199, ELM 251 -> 266. - MASTU15 (documented poor case, do not quote; 5624 -> 19061): Q 1.212e-4 -> 1.196e-4, T_ped 0.1035 -> 0.0931 keV, V_loop 0.283 -> 0.344 V, ELM 298 -> 327, sawtooth 33 -> 34, ends as scheduled in both.

Convergence of the new scheme, measured by the lane on its own base and not repeated here (ITER15, 400 s, flat-top means, gridPacking 4, nRho 50, rtol 1e-2, dtMax 0.5 s): between nRho 50 and 100 Q, f_bs, l_i and T_ped change by 0.02, 0.29, 0.04 and 0.19 % (the uniform grid: 1.6, 0.1, 1.7 and 3.4 %); between the tolerances 1e-2 and 1e-3 or 1e-4 by at most 0.5 %; between the step limits 0.5 s and 0.01 s by at most 0.5 %; the ELM count is 1326, 1326 and 1331 over the step limits. q(0) and the sawtooth count are the least converged numbers of the record (a 0.1 % change of the initial current moves the ITER15 flat-top q(0) by -14 % and the crash count 21 -> 26 on the lane's base): do not quote flat-top q(0) or crash counts of the 1.5D golden cases as converged physics. The cost: ITER15 400 s about 42 s of CPU time instead of about 10 s.

## 2026-09-29 16:40 UTC — v4.0 Wave 2B, lane ws2d: 0D density controller with actuator-lag prediction, published radial builds, design pulses and solenoids of ITER, DEMO and SPARC, TF nuclear heating from the neutron power

Causes. (1) The 0D fuelling command is a new controller (confinement/densityControl.ts): feedback on the density predicted one actuator lag ahead, feed-forward of the losses of the moment, of the beams and of the set-point ramp (half a lag of lead), replacing a proportional command whose loop had a damping ratio of 0.4. The density now follows the ramp of n_target about 6 % more closely (ITER at 9 s: 0.528 against 0.495e20) and sits exactly on it in the flat top (n_e/n_target 0.999 to 1.003, it was 0.97 to 0.98 in H-mode and 1.00 in DIII-D); every 0D magnetic case moves through its density (line-average n/n_G +1.4 % ITER, +2.4 % DEMO, +3.0 % JET, +2.6 % JT-60SA, +3.6 % SPARC, -0.4 % DIII-D, -2.5 % MAST-U, -1.0 % W7-X). No unexplained change: the 1.5D cases do not use this controller and move only in their engineering keys (2). (2) presets.ts: SPARC inboard TF leg 0.325 m and gap 0.22 m (Creely et al. 2020, Figure 2 of the V2 cross-section; 21.3 T on the conductor against the approximately 22 T of Hartwig et al. 2024), DEMO leg 1.3 m and gap 1.75 m with 16 coils (Federici et al. 2019, 2018 baseline: 12.1 T on the conductor, 660 MPa), design pulses (ITER 500 s, DEMO 7200 s, SPARC 31 s) and solenoids (ITER 1.3 to 2.08 m at 13 T; DEMO 13 T with 320 V s from the PF system; SPARC 0.405 to 0.681 m); the systems keys of the shot report follow: TF stress, TF mass, cryo pulse length, cryoplant power, CS flux swing and margin. The CS swing and margin are published only for a preset that gives its solenoid (JET, DIII-D, JT-60SA, MAST-U: keys removed). (3) The TF nuclear heating takes the neutron power (0.80 of P_fus in D-T; D-He3 and p-B11 heat the coil accordingly) and counts armour, first wall, shield and vessel with the shield coefficient in a build without a breeding blanket. (4) MagneticModel.geometryInfo() reports kappaB and deltaB (the LCFS values) beside the nominal 95 % ones, and the golden geometry section stores what it reports: two keys added in the geometry section of every 0D magnetic case (ITER kappaB 1.85, deltaB 0.49).

Headline moves against the previous head. ITER: Q_sci_max 12.15 -> 12.74, flat-top P_fus 513.7 -> 516.6 MW, Q 10.04 -> 10.08, L-H at 9.40 -> 9.64 s (cause 1); TF nuclear heating 44.5 -> 44.8 kW, cryo pulse 1055 -> 500 s, cryoplant 32.6 -> 35.6 MW, net P_el 13 -> 11 MW, flux margin -0.231 -> -0.035 (cause 2). DEMO: flat-top P_fus 1836 -> 1912 MW (+4.2 %), Q 18.27 -> 19.03, ELM count 1788 -> 1812 (cause 1); TF stress 745 -> 570 MPa and the permanent TF coil stress warning is gone (warnings 2 -> 1), 18 -> 16 coils, TF mass 13.5 -> 15.9 kt, cryo pulse 1055 -> 7200 s, cryoplant 30.6 -> 35.7 MW, TF nuclear heating 12.2 -> 42.4 kW, flux margin 0.624 -> 1.291 (cause 2). SPARC: flat-top P_fus 197.3 -> 205.0 MW (+3.9 %), Q_sci_max 9.31 -> 10.15, Q_sci_avg 6.64 -> 6.93, the burn_end event is gone (Q stays above 1 to the end), n/n_G 0.378 -> 0.392 (cause 1); TF stress 1024 -> 1299 MPa (the warning stays: the model is a free cylinder, the coil is bucked, see the preset), TF mass 506 -> 385 t, cryo pulse 1055 -> 31 s, cryoplant 28.2 -> 36.0 MW, TF nuclear heating 476 -> 589 kW, CS swing 8.7 -> 43.5 V s, margin -0.765 -> +0.172 (cause 2 and 3). JET: flat-top P_fus 12.88 -> 12.74 MW, Q_sci_max 0.481 -> 0.476, n/n_G 0.566 -> 0.582 (cause 1). DIII-D: Q_sci_max 3.38e-4 -> 3.46e-4, Tmax 4.94 -> 5.21 keV (cause 1). JT-60SA: n/n_G 0.519 -> 0.533, Q -0.1 % (cause 1). MAST-U: Q_sci_max 1.00e-4 -> 1.04e-4, Tmax 1.86 -> 1.96 keV (cause 1). W7-X: n/n_G 0.963 -> 0.953, Q +0.1 % (cause 1). ITER-DHe3: Q_sci_max 0.0223 -> 0.0237, flat-top H fraction 1 -> 0.75 with 8 L-H and 7 H-L transitions instead of one (a near-threshold plasma that dithers when the density follows the ramp: cause 1; cryo and flux keys cause 2). ITER-pB11 (the marginal case pinned at n_target 1.0e20): collapse at 27.6 -> 29.7 s, flat-top P_fus 9.5e-6 -> 5.1e-6 MW (a burn of micro-watts; the window is the shorter run up to the collapse), n/n_G 0.800 -> 0.812 (cause 1). 1.5D cases (ITER15, JET15, SPARC15, SPARC15-short, SPARC15-DHe3, SPARC15-pB11, DEMO15, DIIID15): only engineering keys move, none of the plasma keys (cause 2 and 3): SPARC15 TF stress 1024 -> 1299 MPa and cryo pulse 31 s, CS margin -0.756 -> +0.218, ITER15 CS margin -0.138 -> +0.083, DEMO15 TF stress 745 -> 570 MPa with 16 coils and the warning removed, DIII-D15 and JET15 lose the CS swing and margin keys. Unchanged: NIF, DIRECT, Z, GF, FRXL, ZAP, TAE, MIRROR, MUON, TAE-pB11, MIRROR-DHe3, MASTU15.

## 2026-09-29 16:23 UTC — ws6b: two golden cases for the opt-in predictive transport closures, 'bgb' (mixed Bohm/gyro-Bohm, Erba et al.) and 'ifspppl' (IFS-PPPL, Kotschenreuther et al.); no existing case moves

Cause. Nothing that a default shot runs changed: the closures are selected only by profiles.transportModel = 'bgb' or 'ifspppl' (the default stays 'scaling'), the new diagnostics H98y2 and HITPA20 are written only when the transport model is predictive, and the new TransportModel.prepare hook and TransportModel.preferredSolver are unused by 'scaling' and 'cgm'. So the 30 recorded cases match at 1e-9 (checked with npm run golden before this record) and only the two added files are new. GoldenCase.overrides gets a transportModel field for the two cases, which no preset uses.

ITER15-bgb: ITER15 with transportModel 'bgb' to 16 s (the ramp-up, the L-H transition at about 10 s, one sawtooth; the 'auto' nonlinear solver is the Pereverzev-Corrigan Picard iteration for this closure). Flat-top means of the last 30 % of the shortened shot: Q 2.50, P_fus 123 MW, W 136 MJ, tau_E 2.65 s (IPB98(y,2) scaling 4.14 s), H98(y,2) 0.64, H(ITPA20) 0.69, T_e0 21.8 keV, T_ped 1.61 keV, alpha_ped/alpha_crit 0.18: the barrier of the preset (etbFactor 0.08) is not strong enough to bring the predicted pedestal to the ballooning limit on ITER (docs: the pedestal, not the closure, is what keeps H98 below 1 there).

JET15-ifspppl: JET15 with transportModel 'ifspppl' to 1.5 s (ramp-up, the L-H transition at about 0.3 s, four sawteeth; no ELM yet). Flat-top means: Q 0.176, P_fus 5.9 MW, W 4.4 MJ, tau_E 0.132 s (scaling 0.259 s), H98(y,2) 0.43, H(ITPA20) 0.45, T_e0 6.2 keV, T_i0 4.1 keV, T_ped 0.93 keV, alpha_ped/alpha_crit 0.95. The IFS-PPPL fit was made for circular L-mode plasmas (its authors: 'cannot yet be used to compare quantitatively to most H modes'), and its confinement is set by the temperature at r/a = 0.8, here the pedestal of the model.

## 2026-09-29 16:27 UTC — ws6c: new golden case SPARC15-redl, SPARC15 (1.5D, 8.7 MA, full shot) with profiles.neoclassicalModel 'redl' The Redl, Angioni, Belli and Sauter coefficients of the bootstrap current and of the neoclassical conductivity (Phys. Plasmas 28 (2021) 022502, eqs. 10-21; current/redl.ts) are an opt-in module beside the Sauter coefficients (default, unchanged); the case pins the option so that a change of the coefficient set, of the way it is selected or of the current sources it feeds is seen. Recorded on the code of the ws6c lane after the change of the bootstrap and conductivity selection, together with the conservative remap at the adoption of an equilibrium and the I_p trigger of the equilibrium update (same physics as the re-record of the existing cases that follows).

## 2026-09-29 16:30 UTC — ws6c re-record: conservative remap of the state at the adoption of a Grad-Shafranov equilibrium, update on a change of I_p, flux ledger with the boundary loop voltage (1.5D), f_bs/f_NI/V_loop of the 0D circuit, CS flux report keys only for a designed solenoid. Moved cases: the eight 1.5D cases, the 0D magnetic cases (new and removed keys only, no existing number moves); pulsed, ICF, FRC, mirror, muon and W7-X cases are unchanged.

(A) 0D magnetic cases (ITER, JET, SPARC, DIIID, JT60SA, MASTU, DEMO, ITER-DHe3, ITER-pB11): no existing key moves, bit for bit. Added: f_bs (Wilson, Nucl. Fusion 32 (1992) 257), f_cd (0, the 0D model has no current drive), f_NI and V_loop = (1 - f_NI) P_oh / I_p (confinement/circuit.ts, PlasmaCircuit) as frame keys, so history.{f_bs,f_cd,f_NI,V_loop}.* and flatTop.{f_bs,f_cd,f_NI,V_loop} appear (ITER flat top: f_bs 0.176, V_loop 0.084 V; JET 0.132 V; the first frame carries the loop voltage of the cold start, 2.7 V on ITER). Removed: scalars.engineering 'CS flux swing (V s)', 'Flux required (V s)' and 'Flux margin' of the presets that carried them (ITER, JET, SPARC, DIIID, JT60SA, DEMO, ITER-DHe3, ITER-pB11): the radial build of a preset does not resolve the solenoid (ITER: TF inner radius 2.0 m against a CS outer radius of 2.08 m) and the margin was negative for every preset but DEMO; the keys are published only when the design gives systems.cs (the report says 'CS flux budget: not evaluated' otherwise). The Ejima coefficient default is 0.4 (PROCESS; the ITER design value 0.45 is systems.cs.ejima), documented in confinement/circuit.ts.

(B) 1.5D cases, the physics: (1) when a new equilibrium replaces the geometry the state is remapped conservatively (coupling/remap.ts): n_e scales with the cell volumes (particles kept), T unchanged (energy kept), the enclosed current at every face and the boundary flux are kept. This is the limit, for a geometry that changes at one instant, of the V-dot terms of the heat and particle equations and the Phi_b-dot term of the current equation that the model did not have. Before it the ITER15 stored energy jumped by +0.07 to +0.35 % at the adoptions of the ramp-up and by -1.3 to -1.9 % (particles -0.5 to -0.7 %) at five of six adoptions after 42 s with no source, and the first step after an adoption took a loop voltage of 31 V at t = 0.8 s and 1 to 3.5 V late in the flat top (0.02 to 0.03 V before it); now particles are kept to 1e-5 % and the energy to 0.025 %. (2) An update is due at once when |I_p - I_p,eq|/I_p,eq exceeds 10 % (EQ_IP_TRIGGER; a ramp of I_p no longer runs on a geometry up to an update interval old); no golden case ramps I_p, so this moves nothing here. (3) V_loop is the voltage at the plasma boundary, 2 pi d(psi_b)/dt with the boundary gradient of the I_p condition, integrated over the accepted steps (current/flux.ts), not the flux of the last cell centre; new keys f_NI, V_res, psi_used, psi_res, psi_ind. The flux drawn from the solenoid closes to 1 % against the resistive flux plus the change of the field energy (the Poynting identity, tests against the Bessel series of the skin-time response).

(C) Headline moves of the flat top (old to new): ITER15 Q 10.697 to 10.715 (+0.17 %), P_fus +0.17 %, W +0.08 %, T_i +0.08 %, T_ped +0.02 %, f_bs 0.2313 to 0.2315, f_cd 0.1155 to 0.1156, l_i -0.09 %, q95 -0.09 %, beta_N +0.10 %, V_loop 0.0582 to 0.0572 V (-1.8 %, definition); whole-shot E_fusion 219.3 to 214.4 GJ (-2.2 %) and Q_sci_avg 10.96 to 10.71 (-2.3 %) from the start-up and L-H phase (burn_start 4.3 s, L-H 8.1 s; ELM count 1332 to 1327, sawtooth crashes 40 to 42, steps 21562 to 21296). JET15 Q 0.4428 to 0.4437 (+0.2 %), f_bs -0.6 %, V_loop +1.1 %, q0 -5.3 % (sawtooth phase), Q_sci_max 0.932 to 0.906 (-2.8 %), E_fusion -0.2 %. SPARC15 Q 6.290 to 6.289, f_bs -0.9 %, l_i +1.0 %, V_loop +1.7 %, E_fusion -0.08 %. SPARC15-short Q 5.233 to 5.219 (-0.3 %), f_bs -0.9 %, q0 -3.6 %, V_loop 0.0315 to 0.0487 V (a 3 s shot: the ramp-up dominates the mean). DEMO15 Q 23.50 to 23.47 (-0.14 %), f_bs -0.5 %, q0 -8 % (sawtooth phase), V_loop 0.0098 to 0.0111 V, E_fusion -0.23 %, ELM count 1318 to 1316. DIIID15 Q +0.41 %, f_bs -0.9 %, l_i +1.1 %, V_loop +1.9 %, Q_sci_max -1.3 %, sawtooth crashes 0 to 2. MASTU15 Q +0.05 %, f_bs -0.02 %, V_loop -0.6 %, T_max (start-up window excluded) 2.48 to 2.30 keV (-7 %), sawtooth 34 to 35. SPARC15-DHe3 Q +1.2 %, P_fus +1.1 %, V_loop +2.5 %. SPARC15-pB11 Q 4.62e-5 to 5.17e-5 (+11.7 %): the p-11B power balance is marginal, and the L-H history changes (events LH 2 and HL 1 become LH 1: one back-transition of the start-up threshold crossing is gone; the mechanism was not analysed further, the balance is marginal); flat-top T_i +0.5 %, W unchanged. No headline moves by more than 2 % in the flat top of the four reference shots (ITER15, JET15, SPARC15, DEMO15); the moves above 2 % are sawtooth-phase quantities (q0), the V_loop definition, start-up-window and marginal cases.

(D) Not headline but moved: history.V_loop.min/max/mean and every key of the equilibrium digest and the profile samples (2100 to 2350 keys per 1.5D case: the adoption instants and the adopted state differ; ITER15 last equilibrium R_axis 6.378 to 6.336 m, beta_p 0.599 to 0.591, l_i 0.722 to 0.717, q95 3.526 to 3.529); the extremum of the per-step V_loop lands on other steps; scalars.engineering 'CS flux swing (V s)', 'Flux required (V s)' and 'Flux margin' are removed from the 1.5D cases as in (A); the engineering 'Loop voltage (avg., V)' follows V_loop (ITER15 0.058 to 0.057, DEMO15 0.010 to 0.011).

## 2026-09-29 16:00 UTC — ws6e: new golden case ITER15-impurity (ITER15, 60 s, impurityTransport 'facit'); the 30 existing cases are untouched

Cause. The opt-in profile-resolved He ash and impurities (ProfileSettings.impurityTransport 'anomalous' | 'facit', src/physics/profiles/impurity/) get one regression-guarded case: ITER15 with the module on for 60 s (n_He(rho), Be and Ar profiles on the particle solver with the neoclassical D, K, H of FACIT, a set-point controller for the volume-average concentrations, local He ash source, ELM and sawtooth crashes, Z_eff(rho) in the conductivity and the bootstrap current). New keys of the case only (fHe, fHe0, tauHeStar, GammaHe, GammaZ, GammaSeed, cZ, cZ0, cZpeak, cSeed, cSeed0, S_W, mZ and the profiles nHe, nZ, nSeed). Flat-top means of the case: n_He/n_e 3.84 % (tau_He*/tau_E 4.65), Z_eff 1.71, c_Be 2.00 %, c_Ar 0.121 %, Q 12.8; 152 ELMs. Default off (impurityTransport absent or 'legacy') is bit-identical: the state layout, the composition and every checkpoint key of the 30 existing cases are those of before (npm run golden compares all of them).

## 2026-09-29 17:44 UTC — ws6e review fix: new golden case ITER15-impurity-neo (ITER15, 30 s, impurityTransport 'facit' with a weak anomalous transport and no set-point controller); the 30 existing cases and ITER15-impurity are untouched by this entry

Cause. The first review of the ws6e lane found that ITER15-impurity is a weak guard of the FACIT wiring: there the neoclassical D is about 0.004 m^2/s against about 0.3 m^2/s anomalous, and the set-point controller of the volume-average concentration compensates what the convection does, so the anomalous and the facit modes differ by 2 % in Z_eff. The new case switches the controller off (impuritySetpoint 'separatrix', the configured concentration is the separatrix value) and scales the anomalous D and pinch down to 5 % of the electron values (impurityDoverDe 0.05, impurityPinchOverPe 0.05), so that the neoclassical D, K, H decide the profiles and a sign, mapping, temperature or geometry error in the wiring moves the result visibly. Flat-top means of the case against the same shot with the anomalous closure alone (impurityTransport 'anomalous'): Z_eff 1.215 against 1.348, c_Be 0.58 % against 0.90 %, c_Ar 0.032 % against 0.054 %, Q 9.6 against 9.0, n_He/n_e 3.65 % against 3.76 %; the Ar profile of the last frames is hollow with FACIT (0.7e-4 on axis, 4.9e-4 at mid-radius, 1.0e-4 at the edge of n_Ar/n_e) and monotonic without it. New keys of the case only: those of ITER15-impurity (fHe, fHe0, tauHeStar, GammaHe, GammaZ, GammaSeed, cZ, cZ0, cZpeak, cSeed, cSeed0, S_W, mZ and the profiles nHe, nZ, nSeed).

Node v24.19.0 · `npm run golden:update` · --only ITER15-impurity-neo

- Added (1): ITER15-impurity-neo

## 2026-09-29 17:49 UTC — ws6e review fix: re-record of the lane's own case ITER15-impurity (recorded earlier in this lane, not yet on the integration branch); the 30 existing cases and ITER15-impurity-neo are unchanged by this entry

Cause. One code change, commit f61fcc5: the main-ion density that FACIT reads on an inner face is interpolated with faceValue (the weights of the edge-packed grid, gridPacking 4, the default), like the ion and electron temperatures, the impurity density and Z_eff of the same face, instead of the plain mean of the two cells. On the packed grid the two cells that share a face differ in width, so the plain mean was off by the weight; N_i enters the Braginskii times and the viscosities of FACIT, so D, K and H move in the last digits and the ELM and sawtooth phase of the shot follows chaotically. No other change touches the case: the wiring tests, the README and the new golden case ITER15-impurity-neo leave the numbers of the model alone; the default (impurityTransport absent or 'legacy') is unaffected because the composition and the FACIT table exist only when the module is on (all 30 existing cases match bit for bit). Headline moves (flat-top frame means unless noted): Q 12.828 -> 12.820 (-0.06 %), P_fus 642.63 -> 642.22 MW, n_He/n_e 3.8445 % -> 3.8440 %, tau_He* 11.329 -> 11.332 s, tau_E 2.4362 -> 2.4368 s, Z_eff 1.7066 -> 1.7065, c_Be 2.0025 % -> 2.0019 %, c_Ar 0.12075 % -> 0.12077 %, set-point multiplier mZ 2.069 -> 2.067, T_e(0) -0.03 %, f_bs -0.04 %, P_rad -0.05 %; GammaZ (the mean net Be influx through the separatrix, 1e20/s) 0.108 -> 0.088 and GammaSeed (Ar) 0.0913 -> 0.0794, the small difference of large fluxes that the set-point controller balances, and V_loop 34.59 -> 33.31 mV (-3.7 %), all of them within the ELM cycle scatter; ELMs 152 -> 152, frames 943 -> 938, accepted steps 3112 -> 3103, Q_sci_max 13.135 -> 13.203; last-frame equilibrium (the ELM phase at the end of the shot): beta_p 0.6894 -> 0.6952, l_i 0.7399 -> 0.7323, q95 3.553 -> 3.596, R_axis +1.3e-4. Nothing moved that was not traced to the interpolation.

## 2026-09-29 16:44 UTC — WS6a: new golden case ITER15-EPED (EPED1-type pedestal and Loarte ELM energy loss switched on); no existing case moves

ITER15-EPED is the ITER15 preset shortened to 30 s with ProfileSettings.pedestalModel = 'eped1' and elmLoss = 'loarte' (profiles/pedestal/): the pedestal width from the KBM constraint 0.076 sqrt(beta_p,ped), the pedestal-top pressure limit from the peeling-ballooning height anchored to the DIII-D fit of Groebner et al. (GA-A26243), an adaptive transport barrier that holds the pedestal at that limit, an ELM when the pressure reaches it, and the ELM energy f(nu*_ped) W_ped of Loarte et al. (2003). L-H at about 8 s, ELMs from about 12 s. It guards the two opt-in modules (the default path is guarded by the other 30 cases, all unchanged by this lane: golden compare before and after the lane passes at 1e-9 on all 30).

Added with --only ITER15-EPED; the other files are untouched.

## 2026-09-29 17:46 UTC — WS6d: three new golden cases for the opt-in physics of the lane (fast-ion energy fields, physics-based current drive, Porcelli sawtooth trigger with the helical-flux reset). No existing case moved: all 30 earlier cases matched at 1e-9 with the WS6d code in the tree before these were recorded (the new settings default to the scalar fast-ion pools, the legacy current-drive scalings, the shear trigger and the q >= 1.01 rebuild).

Headline values of the new cases (flat-top means over the short run): JET15-fast (JET15, 1.5 s, fastIonModel 'profile', cdModel 'physics'): Q 0.570, P_fus 18.8 MW, W 8.32 MJ, fast-ion energy 3.12 MJ, beta_N 1.68, f_bs 0.25, f_cd 0.56 (I_nbcd 1.97 MA at the low start-up density, the current profile is hollowed, q0 44), 1264 steps; relative to the default JET15 shot the fields deliver the heating with the slowing-down delay, smooth the source over the orbit width and put the fast pressure into the equilibrium table and the ballooning drive. DIIID15-eccd (DIII-D 1.5D, 1.5 s, cdModel 'physics', ECCD launcher aimed at rho 0.35 with n_par 0.35): I_nbcd 0.66 MA (80 keV beam), I_eccd 0.022 MA for 3 MW (zeta about 0.04 at the low-field-side absorption, the Ohkawa reduction), f_cd 0.43, 3824 steps. MASTU15-saw (MAST-U 1.5D, 0.5 s, sawtoothTrigger 'porcelli', sawtoothReconnection 'kadomtsev'): 3 sawtooth crashes at 0.32, 0.38 and 0.46 s (the shear trigger of MASTU15 crashes at the 50 ms refractory time), q0 after a crash 1.00, 3933 steps.

The cases are regression guards of the new code paths, not validated predictions: the NBCD magnitude follows the beam pitch (nbiRtan is one value for all presets), and full reconnection leaves q0 = 1.00.
